.env
ENV=production
BACKEND_URL=http://127.0.0.1:8000

SESSION_SECRET=autonomous-platform-secret
SESSION_EXP_MINUTES=480

AUTO_APPROVE_LOW=true
ENABLE_REAL_REMEDIATION=false
DEFAULT_NAMESPACE=default

CPU_THRESHOLD=85
ERROR_RATE_THRESHOLD=5
LATENCY_MS_THRESHOLD=1500

backend/main.py
import json
import os
import sqlite3
import subprocess
import time
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional

import jwt
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from fastapi import Depends, FastAPI, Header, HTTPException, Response
from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
from prometheus_client import Counter, Histogram, generate_latest

# ---------------------------------------------------------
# CONFIG
# ---------------------------------------------------------
APP_TITLE = "Autonomous Platform Control Plane"
APP_VERSION = "1.0.0"

BASE_DIR = Path(".")
DATA_DIR = BASE_DIR / "data"
DATA_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

DB_PATH = DATA_DIR / "autonomous.db"
AUDIT_LOG_PATH = DATA_DIR / "audit_log.jsonl"

SESSION_SECRET = os.getenv("SESSION_SECRET", "autonomous-platform-secret")
SESSION_EXP_MINUTES = int(os.getenv("SESSION_EXP_MINUTES", "480"))

AUTO_APPROVE_LOW = os.getenv("AUTO_APPROVE_LOW", "true").lower() == "true"
ENABLE_REAL_REMEDIATION = os.getenv("ENABLE_REAL_REMEDIATION", "false").lower() == "true"
DEFAULT_NAMESPACE = os.getenv("DEFAULT_NAMESPACE", "default")

CPU_THRESHOLD = float(os.getenv("CPU_THRESHOLD", "85"))
ERROR_RATE_THRESHOLD = float(os.getenv("ERROR_RATE_THRESHOLD", "5"))
LATENCY_MS_THRESHOLD = float(os.getenv("LATENCY_MS_THRESHOLD", "1500"))

# ---------------------------------------------------------
# METRICS
# ---------------------------------------------------------
REQUEST_COUNT = Counter(
    "http_requests_total",
    "Total HTTP Requests",
    ["method", "endpoint", "status"]
)

REQUEST_LATENCY = Histogram(
    "http_request_duration_seconds",
    "Request latency",
    ["endpoint"]
)

AUTONOMOUS_EVALUATIONS = Counter(
    "autonomous_evaluations_total",
    "Number of autonomous evaluation cycles"
)

INCIDENTS_RAISED = Counter(
    "autonomous_incidents_total",
    "Incidents raised by severity",
    ["severity"]
)

ACTIONS_CREATED = Counter(
    "autonomous_actions_total",
    "Actions created by type",
    ["action_type"]
)

ACTIONS_EXECUTED = Counter(
    "autonomous_actions_executed_total",
    "Actions executed by result",
    ["result"]
)

# ---------------------------------------------------------
# AUTH / RBAC
# ---------------------------------------------------------
DEMO_USERS = {
    "admin": "admin",
    "reviewer": "compliance_reviewer",
    "operator": "operator",
    "viewer": "viewer",
}

def create_token(user: str, role: str) -> str:
    payload = {
        "sub": user,
        "role": role,
        "exp": datetime.utcnow() + timedelta(minutes=SESSION_EXP_MINUTES)
    }
    return jwt.encode(payload, SESSION_SECRET, algorithm="HS256")

def decode_token(token: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    try:
        return jwt.decode(token, SESSION_SECRET, algorithms=["HS256"])
    except Exception:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Invalid or expired session token")

def require_session(x_session_token: Optional[str] = Header(default=None)) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    if not x_session_token:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Missing X-Session-Token")
    return decode_token(x_session_token)

def require_roles(session: Dict[str, Any], allowed_roles: List[str]):
    if session["role"] not in allowed_roles:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="Access denied")

# ---------------------------------------------------------
# DATABASE
# ---------------------------------------------------------
def get_conn():
    conn = sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH)
    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
    return conn

def init_db():
    conn = get_conn()
    cur = conn.cursor()

    cur.execute("""
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS telemetry (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
            service TEXT NOT NULL,
            metric_name TEXT NOT NULL,
            metric_value REAL NOT NULL,
            ts TEXT NOT NULL
        )
    """)

    cur.execute("""
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS incidents (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
            service TEXT NOT NULL,
            incident_type TEXT NOT NULL,
            severity TEXT NOT NULL,
            evidence_json TEXT NOT NULL,
            diagnosis TEXT NOT NULL,
            status TEXT NOT NULL,
            created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
            closed_at TEXT
        )
    """)

    cur.execute("""
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS actions (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
            incident_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
            service TEXT NOT NULL,
            action_type TEXT NOT NULL,
            parameters_json TEXT NOT NULL,
            approval_required INTEGER NOT NULL,
            approval_status TEXT NOT NULL,
            execution_status TEXT NOT NULL,
            created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
            reviewed_by TEXT,
            reviewed_at TEXT,
            executed_by TEXT,
            executed_at TEXT,
            result_json TEXT
        )
    """)

    conn.commit()
    conn.close()

init_db()

# ---------------------------------------------------------
# MODELS
# ---------------------------------------------------------
class TelemetryIn(BaseModel):
    service: str = Field(..., min_length=1)
    metric_name: str = Field(..., min_length=1)
    metric_value: float
    ts: Optional[str] = None

class BulkTelemetryIn(BaseModel):
    items: List[TelemetryIn]

class ApprovalIn(BaseModel):
    decision: str = Field(..., pattern="^(approved|rejected)$")
    comment: str = ""

class ExecuteIn(BaseModel):
    force: bool = False

# ---------------------------------------------------------
# AUDIT
# ---------------------------------------------------------
def audit(event_type: str, actor: str, details: Dict[str, Any]):
    row = {
        "timestamp": datetime.utcnow().isoformat(),
        "event_type": event_type,
        "actor": actor,
        "details": details,
    }
    with open(AUDIT_LOG_PATH, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.write(json.dumps(row, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")

# ---------------------------------------------------------
# UTILS
# ---------------------------------------------------------
def now_iso() -> str:
    return datetime.utcnow().isoformat()

def insert_telemetry(item: TelemetryIn):
    conn = get_conn()
    cur = conn.cursor()
    cur.execute(
        "INSERT INTO telemetry(service, metric_name, metric_value, ts) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)",
        (item.service, item.metric_name, item.metric_value, item.ts or now_iso())
    )
    conn.commit()
    conn.close()

def fetch_recent_telemetry(minutes: int = 60) -> pd.DataFrame:
    conn = get_conn()
    cur = conn.cursor()
    cutoff = (datetime.utcnow() - timedelta(minutes=minutes)).isoformat()
    cur.execute("SELECT * FROM telemetry WHERE ts >= ? ORDER BY ts ASC", (cutoff,))
    rows = cur.fetchall()
    conn.close()
    if not rows:
        return pd.DataFrame(columns=["id", "service", "metric_name", "metric_value", "ts"])
    df = pd.DataFrame([dict(r) for r in rows])
    return df

def unresolved_incident_exists(service: str, incident_type: str) -> bool:
    conn = get_conn()
    cur = conn.cursor()
    cur.execute(
        """
        SELECT id FROM incidents
        WHERE service = ? AND incident_type = ? AND status IN ('open', 'under_review')
        ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1
        """,
        (service, incident_type)
    )
    row = cur.fetchone()
    conn.close()
    return row is not None

def create_incident(service: str, incident_type: str, severity: str, evidence: Dict[str, Any], diagnosis: str) -> int:
    conn = get_conn()
    cur = conn.cursor()
    cur.execute(
        """
        INSERT INTO incidents(service, incident_type, severity, evidence_json, diagnosis, status, created_at)
        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
        """,
        (service, incident_type, severity, json.dumps(evidence, ensure_ascii=False), diagnosis, "open", now_iso())
    )
    incident_id = cur.lastrowid
    conn.commit()
    conn.close()
    INCIDENTS_RAISED.labels(severity=severity).inc()
    return int(incident_id)

def create_action(incident_id: int, service: str, action_type: str, parameters: Dict[str, Any], approval_required: bool) -> int:
    approval_status = "pending" if approval_required else "approved"
    conn = get_conn()
    cur = conn.cursor()
    cur.execute(
        """
        INSERT INTO actions(
            incident_id, service, action_type, parameters_json,
            approval_required, approval_status, execution_status, created_at
        )
        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
        """,
        (
            incident_id,
            service,
            action_type,
            json.dumps(parameters, ensure_ascii=False),
            1 if approval_required else 0,
            approval_status,
            "ready" if not approval_required else "awaiting_approval",
            now_iso(),
        )
    )
    action_id = cur.lastrowid
    conn.commit()
    conn.close()
    ACTIONS_CREATED.labels(action_type=action_type).inc()
    return int(action_id)

def get_incidents() -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    conn = get_conn()
    cur = conn.cursor()
    cur.execute("SELECT * FROM incidents ORDER BY id DESC")
    rows = cur.fetchall()
    conn.close()
    return [
        {
            "id": r["id"],
            "service": r["service"],
            "incident_type": r["incident_type"],
            "severity": r["severity"],
            "evidence": json.loads(r["evidence_json"]),
            "diagnosis": r["diagnosis"],
            "status": r["status"],
            "created_at": r["created_at"],
            "closed_at": r["closed_at"],
        }
        for r in rows
    ]

def get_actions() -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    conn = get_conn()
    cur = conn.cursor()
    cur.execute("SELECT * FROM actions ORDER BY id DESC")
    rows = cur.fetchall()
    conn.close()
    return [
        {
            "id": r["id"],
            "incident_id": r["incident_id"],
            "service": r["service"],
            "action_type": r["action_type"],
            "parameters": json.loads(r["parameters_json"]),
            "approval_required": bool(r["approval_required"]),
            "approval_status": r["approval_status"],
            "execution_status": r["execution_status"],
            "created_at": r["created_at"],
            "reviewed_by": r["reviewed_by"],
            "reviewed_at": r["reviewed_at"],
            "executed_by": r["executed_by"],
            "executed_at": r["executed_at"],
            "result": json.loads(r["result_json"]) if r["result_json"] else {},
        }
        for r in rows
    ]

def update_action_approval(action_id: int, reviewer: str, decision: str, comment: str):
    conn = get_conn()
    cur = conn.cursor()
    next_exec_status = "ready" if decision == "approved" else "cancelled"
    cur.execute(
        """
        UPDATE actions
        SET approval_status = ?, execution_status = ?, reviewed_by = ?, reviewed_at = ?, result_json = ?
        WHERE id = ?
        """,
        (
            decision,
            next_exec_status,
            reviewer,
            now_iso(),
            json.dumps({"review_comment": comment}, ensure_ascii=False),
            action_id,
        )
    )
    conn.commit()
    conn.close()

def update_action_execution(action_id: int, executor: str, execution_status: str, result: Dict[str, Any]):
    conn = get_conn()
    cur = conn.cursor()
    cur.execute(
        """
        UPDATE actions
        SET execution_status = ?, executed_by = ?, executed_at = ?, result_json = ?
        WHERE id = ?
        """,
        (
            execution_status,
            executor,
            now_iso(),
            json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False),
            action_id,
        )
    )
    conn.commit()
    conn.close()

def close_incident(incident_id: int):
    conn = get_conn()
    cur = conn.cursor()
    cur.execute(
        "UPDATE incidents SET status = ?, closed_at = ? WHERE id = ?",
        ("closed", now_iso(), incident_id)
    )
    conn.commit()
    conn.close()

# ---------------------------------------------------------
# ANALYTICS
# ---------------------------------------------------------
def latest_value(service_df: pd.DataFrame, metric_name: str) -> Optionalmetric_df = service_df[service_df["metric_name"] == metric_name]
    if metric_df.empty:
        return None
    return float(metric_df.iloc[-1]["metric_value"])

def moving_average(service_df: pd.DataFrame, metric_name: str, last_n: int = 5) -> Optionalmetric_df = service_df[service_df["metric_name"] == metric_name]
    if metric_df.empty:
        return None
    return float(metric_df["metric_value"].tail(last_n).mean())

def classify_service(service_df: pd.DataFrame) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    service = service_df["service"].iloc[0]

    cpu = latest_value(service_df, "cpu_percent")
    error_rate = latest_value(service_df, "error_rate")
    latency = latest_value(service_df, "latency_ms")

    cpu_avg = moving_average(service_df, "cpu_percent")
    error_avg = moving_average(service_df, "error_rate")
    latency_avg = moving_average(service_df, "latency_ms")

    incidents = []

    if cpu is not None and cpu >= CPU_THRESHOLD:
        severity = "low" if cpu < 95 else "medium"
        incidents.append({
            "service": service,
            "incident_type": "cpu_saturation",
            "severity": severity,
            "evidence": {
                "cpu_percent": cpu,
                "cpu_avg": cpu_avg,
                "threshold": CPU_THRESHOLD,
            },
            "diagnosis": f"CPU saturation detected for {service}. Latest CPU {cpu}%, threshold {CPU_THRESHOLD}%."
        })

    if error_rate is not None and error_rate >= ERROR_RATE_THRESHOLD:
        severity = "medium" if error_rate < 10 else "high"
        incidents.append({
            "service": service,
            "incident_type": "error_rate_spike",
            "severity": severity,
            "evidence": {
                "error_rate": error_rate,
                "error_avg": error_avg,
                "threshold": ERROR_RATE_THRESHOLD,
            },
            "diagnosis": f"Error rate spike detected for {service}. Latest error rate {error_rate}%, threshold {ERROR_RATE_THRESHOLD}%."
        })

    if latency is not None and latency >= LATENCY_MS_THRESHOLD:
        severity = "low" if latency < 2500 else "medium"
        incidents.append({
            "service": service,
            "incident_type": "latency_degradation",
            "severity": severity,
            "evidence": {
                "latency_ms": latency,
                "latency_avg": latency_avg,
                "threshold": LATENCY_MS_THRESHOLD,
            },
            "diagnosis": f"Latency degradation detected for {service}. Latest latency {latency} ms, threshold {LATENCY_MS_THRESHOLD} ms."
        })

    # compounded failure
    if error_rate is not None and latency is not None:
        if error_rate >= ERROR_RATE_THRESHOLD and latency >= LATENCY_MS_THRESHOLD:
            incidents.append({
                "service": service,
                "incident_type": "release_regression_suspected",
                "severity": "high",
                "evidence": {
                    "error_rate": error_rate,
                    "latency_ms": latency,
                    "error_threshold": ERROR_RATE_THRESHOLD,
                    "latency_threshold": LATENCY_MS_THRESHOLD,
                },
                "diagnosis": f"Combined error-rate and latency regression suspected for {service}; recommended rollback candidate."
            })

    return incidents

def plan_action(incident: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    incident_type = incident["incident_type"]
    service = incident["service"]
    severity = incident["severity"]

    approval_required = False

    if severity in ["medium", "high"]:
        approval_required = True

    if severity == "low" and AUTO_APPROVE_LOW:
        approval_required = False

    if incident_type == "cpu_saturation":
        return {
            "service": service,
            "action_type": "scale_up",
            "parameters": {"deployment": service, "replicas_delta": 1, "namespace": DEFAULT_NAMESPACE},
            "approval_required": approval_required
        }

    if incident_type == "error_rate_spike":
        return {
            "service": service,
            "action_type": "restart_service",
            "parameters": {"deployment": service, "namespace": DEFAULT_NAMESPACE},
            "approval_required": approval_required
        }

    if incident_type == "latency_degradation":
        return {
            "service": service,
            "action_type": "scale_up",
            "parameters": {"deployment": service, "replicas_delta": 1, "namespace": DEFAULT_NAMESPACE},
            "approval_required": approval_required
        }

    if incident_type == "release_regression_suspected":
        return {
            "service": service,
            "action_type": "rollback_release",
            "parameters": {"deployment": service, "namespace": DEFAULT_NAMESPACE},
            "approval_required": True
        }

    return {
        "service": service,
        "action_type": "observe_only",
        "parameters": {"deployment": service, "namespace": DEFAULT_NAMESPACE},
        "approval_required": True
    }

# ---------------------------------------------------------
# REMEDIATION
# ---------------------------------------------------------
def run_command(cmd: List[str]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    try:
        proc = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, check=True)
        return {
            "success": True,
            "stdout": proc.stdout,
            "stderr": proc.stderr,
            "cmd": cmd,
        }
    except subprocess.CalledProcessError as e:
        return {
            "success": False,
            "stdout": e.stdout,
            "stderr": e.stderr,
            "cmd": cmd,
        }

def execute_remediation(action: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    action_type = action["action_type"]
    params = action["parameters"]
    deployment = params.get("deployment", "")
    namespace = params.get("namespace", DEFAULT_NAMESPACE)

    if not ENABLE_REAL_REMEDIATION:
        return {
            "mode": "dry_run",
            "success": True,
            "message": f"Simulated {action_type} for deployment {deployment} in namespace {namespace}",
            "parameters": params,
        }

    if action_type == "restart_service":
        cmd = ["kubectl", "rollout", "restart", f"deployment/{deployment}", "-n", namespace]
        return run_command(cmd)

    if action_type == "rollback_release":
        cmd = ["kubectl", "rollout", "undo", f"deployment/{deployment}", "-n", namespace]
        return run_command(cmd)

    if action_type == "scale_up":
        get_cmd = ["kubectl", "get", f"deployment/{deployment}", "-n", namespace, "-o", "jsonpath={.spec.replicas}"]
        current = run_command(get_cmd)
        if not current["success"]:
            return current
        current_replicas = int(current["stdout"] or "1")
        desired = current_replicas + int(params.get("replicas_delta", 1))
        scale_cmd = ["kubectl", "scale", f"deployment/{deployment}", f"--replicas={desired}", "-n", namespace]
        return run_command(scale_cmd)

    return {
        "mode": "noop",
        "success": False,
        "message": f"Unsupported action_type {action_type}",
    }

# APP

app = FastAPI(title=APP_TITLE, version=APP_VERSION)

app.add_middleware(
    CORSMiddleware,
    allow_origins=["*"],
    allow_credentials=True,
    allow_methods=["*"],
    allow_headers=["*"],
)

@app.middleware("http")
async def metrics_middleware(request, call_next):
    start = time.time()
    response = await call_next(request)
    duration = time.time() - start

    REQUEST_COUNT.labels(
        method=request.method,
        endpoint=request.url.path,
        status=response.status_code
    ).inc()

    REQUEST_LATENCY.labels(endpoint=request.url.path).observe(duration)
    return response

# AUTH
# ---------------------------------------------------------
@app.get("/auth/login/{user}")
def login(user: str):
    role = DEMO_USERS.get(user)
    if not role:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="User not found")
    token = create_token(user, role)
    return {"token": token, "user": user, "role": role}

@app.get("/auth/me")
def me(session: Dict[str, Any] = Depends(require_session)):
    return {"user": session["sub"], "role": session["role"]}

        "version": APP_VERSION,
        "mode": "autonomous_prototype",
        "real_remediation_enabled": ENABLE_REAL_REMEDIATION,
        "auto_approve_low": AUTO_APPROVE_LOW,

# TELEMETRY

@app.post("/telemetry")
def ingest_telemetry(item: TelemetryIn, session: Dict[str, Any] = Depends(require_session)):
    require_roles(session, ["admin", "operator", "compliance_reviewer"])
    insert_telemetry(item)
    audit("telemetry_ingest", session["sub"], item.model_dump())
    return {"status": "ingested"}

@app.post("/telemetry/bulk")
def ingest_bulk(payload: BulkTelemetryIn, session: Dict[str, Any] = Depends(require_session)):
    require_roles(session, ["admin", "operator", "compliance_reviewer"])
    for item in payload.items:
        insert_telemetry(item)
    audit("telemetry_bulk_ingest", session["sub"], {"count": len(payload.items)})
    return {"status": "ingested", "count": len(payload.items)}

@app.post("/telemetry/sample")
def generate_sample(session: Dict[str, Any] = Depends(require_session)):
    require_roles(session, ["admin", "operator"])
    rows = [
        TelemetryIn(service="orders-api", metric_name="cpu_percent", metric_value=91),
        TelemetryIn(service="orders-api", metric_name="error_rate", metric_value=7),
        TelemetryIn(service="orders-api", metric_name="latency_ms", metric_value=2200),
        TelemetryIn(service="billing-worker", metric_name="cpu_percent", metric_value=62),
        TelemetryIn(service="billing-worker", metric_name="error_rate", metric_value=1),
        TelemetryIn(service="billing-worker", metric_name="latency_ms", metric_value=420),
        TelemetryIn(service="claims-service", metric_name="cpu_percent", metric_value=87),
        TelemetryIn(service="claims-service", metric_name="error_rate", metric_value=0.5),
        TelemetryIn(service="claims-service", metric_name="latency_ms", metric_value=1600),
    ]
    for item in rows:
        insert_telemetry(item)
    audit("telemetry_sample_generated", session["sub"], {"count": len(rows)})
    return {"status": "generated", "count": len(rows)}

@app.get("/telemetry/recent")
def recent_telemetry(session: Dict[str, Any] = Depends(require_session)):
    df = fetch_recent_telemetry(minutes=240)
    return df.to_dict(orient="records")

# ---------------------------------------------------------
# AUTONOMOUS LOOP
# ---------------------------------------------------------
@app.post("/autonomous/evaluate")
def autonomous_evaluate(session: Dict[str, Any] = Depends(require_session)):
    require_roles(session, ["admin", "operator", "compliance_reviewer"])
    AUTONOMOUS_EVALUATIONS.inc()

    df = fetch_recent_telemetry(minutes=240)
    if df.empty:
        return {"status": "no_data", "created_incidents": 0, "created_actions": 0}

    created_incidents = 0
    created_actions = 0

    for service, service_df in df.groupby("service"):
        incidents = classify_service(service_df)

        for incident in incidents:
            if unresolved_incident_exists(service, incident["incident_type"]):
                continue

            incident_id = create_incident(
                service=incident["service"],
                incident_type=incident["incident_type"],
                severity=incident["severity"],
                evidence=incident["evidence"],
                diagnosis=incident["diagnosis"],
            )
            created_incidents += 1

            planned = plan_action(incident)
            create_action(
                incident_id=incident_id,
                service=planned["service"],
                action_type=planned["action_type"],
                parameters=planned["parameters"],
                approval_required=planned["approval_required"],
            )
            created_actions += 1

            audit(
                "incident_created",
                session["sub"],
                {
                    "incident_id": incident_id,
                    "service": incident["service"],
                    "incident_type": incident["incident_type"],
                    "severity": incident["severity"],
                },
            )

    return {
        "status": "evaluated",
        "created_incidents": created_incidents,
        "created_actions": created_actions,
    }

# ---------------------------------------------------------
# INCIDENTS / ACTIONS
# ---------------------------------------------------------
@app.get("/incidents")
def incidents(session: Dict[str, Any] = Depends(require_session)):
    return get_incidents()

@app.get("/actions")
def actions(session: Dict[str, Any] = Depends(require_session)):
    return get_actions()

@app.post("/actions/{action_id}/approve")
def approve_action(action_id: int, payload: ApprovalIn, session: Dict[str, Any] = Depends(require_session)):
    require_roles(session, ["admin", "compliance_reviewer"])
    update_action_approval(action_id, session["sub"], payload.decision, payload.comment)
    audit("action_reviewed", session["sub"], {"action_id": action_id, "decision": payload.decision, "comment": payload.comment})
    return {"status": payload.decision}

@app.post("/actions/{action_id}/execute")
def execute_action(action_id: int, payload: ExecuteIn, session: Dict[str, Any] = Depends(require_session)):
    require_roles(session, ["admin", "operator", "compliance_reviewer"])

    acts = [a for a in get_actions() if a["id"] == action_id]
    if not acts:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Action not found")
    action = acts[0]

    if action["approval_required"] and action["approval_status"] != "approved" and not payload.force:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Action requires approval")

    result = execute_remediation(action)
    status = "executed" if result.get("success") else "failed"
    update_action_execution(action_id, session["sub"], status, result)
    ACTIONS_EXECUTED.labels(result=status).inc()

    if status == "executed":
        close_incident(action["incident_id"])

    audit("action_executed", session["sub"], {"action_id": action_id, "status": status, "result": result})
    return {"status": status, "result": result}

# ---------------------------------------------------------
# DASHBOARD SUMMARY
# ---------------------------------------------------------
@app.get("/dashboard/summary")
def dashboard_summary(session: Dict[str, Any] = Depends(require_session)):
    incidents = get_incidents()
    actions = get_actions()
    telemetry = fetch_recent_telemetry(minutes=240)

    summary = {
        "total_incidents": len(incidents),
        "open_incidents": sum(1 for i in incidents if i["status"] in ["open", "under_review"]),
        "high_incidents": sum(1 for i in incidents if i["severity"] == "high"),
        "medium_incidents": sum(1 for i in incidents if i["severity"] == "medium"),
        "low_incidents": sum(1 for i in incidents if i["severity"] == "low"),
        "total_actions": len(actions),
        "approved_actions": sum(1 for a in actions if a["approval_status"] == "approved"),
        "pending_actions": sum(1 for a in actions if a["approval_status"] == "pending"),
        "executed_actions": sum(1 for a in actions if a["execution_status"] == "executed"),
        "failed_actions": sum(1 for a in actions if a["execution_status"] == "failed"),
        "services_monitored": int(telemetry["service"].nunique()) if not telemetry.empty else 0,
    }

    return summary

frontend/Home.py
import os
import json
import requests
import pandas as pd
import streamlit as st

BACKEND_URL = os.getenv("BACKEND_URL", "http://127.0.0.1:8000")

def api_get(path: str, token: str = "", params=None, timeout=60):
    headers = {"X-Session-Token": token} if token else {}
    return requests.get(f"{BACKEND_URL}{path}", headers=headers, params=params or {}, timeout=timeout)

def api_post(path: str, token: str = "", json_body=None, timeout=120):
    headers = {"X-Session-Token": token} if token else {}
    return requests.post(f"{BACKEND_URL}{path}", headers=headers, json=json_body, timeout=timeout)

st.set_page_config(page_title="Autonomous Platform", layout="wide")
st.title("Autonomous Platform Control Plane")
st.caption("Deterministic anomaly detection • policy-bound remediation • approvals • metrics")

if "token" not in st.session_state:
    st.session_state["token"] = ""
if "user" not in st.session_state:
    st.session_state["user"] = ""
if "role" not in st.session_state:
    st.session_state["role"] = ""

with st.sidebar:
    st.header("Login")
    selected_user = st.selectbox("User", ["admin", "reviewer", "operator", "viewer"])
    if st.button("Login"):
        r = requests.get(f"{BACKEND_URL}/auth/login/{selected_user}", timeout=30)
        if r.status_code == 200:
            data = r.json()
            st.session_state["token"] = data["token"]
            st.session_state["user"] = data["user"]
            st.session_state["role"] = data["role"]
        else:
            st.error(r.text)

    if st.session_state["token"]:
        st.success(f"{st.session_state['user']} ({st.session_state['role']})")

token = st.session_state["token"]
if not token:
    st.info("Login to continue.")
    st.stop()

tabs = st.tabs(["Overview", "Telemetry", "Incidents & Actions", "Boardroom"])

# ---------------------------------------------------------
# OVERVIEW
# ---------------------------------------------------------
with tabs[0]:
    health = api_get("/health", token=token)
    if health.status_code == 200:
        st.success(json.dumps(health.json(), indent=2))

    summary = api_get("/dashboard/summary", token=token)
    if summary.status_code == 200:
        s = summary.json()
        c1, c2, c3, c4 = st.columns(4)
        c1.metric("Open Incidents", s["open_incidents"])
        c2.metric("High Incidents", s["high_incidents"])
        c3.metric("Pending Actions", s["pending_actions"])
        c4.metric("Executed Actions", s["executed_actions"])

        st.markdown("---")
        st.subheader("Autonomous Loop")
        a1, a2 = st.columns(2)
        with a1:
            if st.button("Generate Sample Telemetry", use_container_width=True):
                r = api_post("/telemetry/sample", token=token, json_body={})
                if r.status_code == 200:
                    st.success(r.json())
                else:
                    st.error(r.text)
        with a2:
            if st.button("Run Autonomous Evaluation", use_container_width=True):
                r = api_post("/autonomous/evaluate", token=token, json_body={})
                if r.status_code == 200:
                    st.success(r.json())
                else:
                    st.error(r.text)

# ---------------------------------------------------------
# TELEMETRY
# ---------------------------------------------------------
with tabsst.subheader("Manual Telemetry Ingestion")

    col1, col2, col3 = st.columns(3)
    with col1:
        service = st.text_input("Service", value="orders-api")
    with col2:
        metric_name = st.selectbox("Metric", ["cpu_percent", "error_rate", "latency_ms"])
    with col3:
        metric_value = st.number_input("Metric value", value=90.0)

    if st.button("Send Telemetry", use_container_width=True):
        payload = {
            "service": service,
            "metric_name": metric_name,
            "metric_value": metric_value
        }
        r = api_post("/telemetry", token=token, json_body=payload)
        if r.status_code == 200:
            st.success(r.json())
        else:
            st.error(r.text)

    st.markdown("---")
    st.subheader("Recent Telemetry")
    t = api_get("/telemetry/recent", token=token)
    if t.status_code == 200:
        df = pd.DataFrame(t.json())
        if not df.empty:
            st.dataframe(df, use_container_width=True, hide_index=True)
            if "ts" in df.columns:
                try:
                    df["ts"] = pd.to_datetime(df["ts"])
                    for svc in df["service"].unique():
                        svc_df = df[df["service"] == svc]
                        for metric in ["cpu_percent", "error_rate", "latency_ms"]:
                            metric_df = svc_df[svc_df["metric_name"] == metric][["ts", "metric_value"]]
                            if not metric_df.empty:
                                st.markdown(f"**{svc} — {metric}**")
                                metric_df = metric_df.set_index("ts")
                                st.line_chart(metric_df)
                except Exception:
                    pass
        else:
            st.info("No telemetry available.")

# ---------------------------------------------------------
# INCIDENTS & ACTIONS
# ---------------------------------------------------------
with tabsst.subheader("Incidents")
    inc = api_get("/incidents", token=token)
    if inc.status_code == 200:
        inc_df = pd.DataFrame(inc.json())
        if not inc_df.empty:
            st.dataframe(inc_df, use_container_width=True, hide_index=True)
        else:
            st.info("No incidents found.")

    st.markdown("---")
    st.subheader("Actions")
    act = api_get("/actions", token=token)
    if act.status_code == 200:
        actions = act.json()
        act_df = pd.DataFrame(actions)
        if not act_df.empty:
            st.dataframe(act_df, use_container_width=True, hide_index=True)

            st.markdown("---")
            st.subheader("Review / Execute")
            action_ids = act_df["id"].tolist()
            selected_action = st.selectbox("Select action", action_ids)

            c1, c2, c3 = st.columns(3)
            with c1:
                if st.button("Approve", use_container_width=True):
                    r = api_post(f"/actions/{selected_action}/approve", token=token, json_body={"decision": "approved", "comment": "Approved from UI"})
                    if r.status_code == 200:
                        st.success(r.json())
                    else:
                        st.error(r.text)
            with c2:
                if st.button("Reject", use_container_width=True):
                    r = api_post(f"/actions/{selected_action}/approve", token=token, json_body={"decision": "rejected", "comment": "Rejected from UI"})
                    if r.status_code == 200:
                        st.warning(r.json())
                    else:
                        st.error(r.text)
            with c3:
                if st.button("Execute", use_container_width=True):
                    r = api_post(f"/actions/{selected_action}/execute", token=token, json_body={"force": False})
                    if r.status_code == 200:
                        st.success(r.json())
                    else:
                        st.error(r.text)
        else:
            st.info("No actions found.")

# ---------------------------------------------------------
# BOARDROOM
# ---------------------------------------------------------
with tabsst.subheader("Boardroom Executive Dashboard")
    summary = api_get("/dashboard/summary", token=token)
    incidents = api_get("/incidents", token=token)
    actions = api_get("/actions", token=token)

    if summary.status_code == 200:
        s = summary.json()
        m1, m2, m3, m4 = st.columns(4)
        m1.metric("Services Monitored", s["services_monitored"])
        m2.metric("Open Incidents", s["open_incidents"])
        m3.metric("Total Actions", s["total_actions"])
        m4.metric("Executed Actions", s["executed_actions"])

    if incidents.status_code == 200:
        inc_df = pd.DataFrame(incidents.json())
        if not inc_df.empty:
            st.markdown("**Incident Severity Distribution**")
            st.bar_chart(inc_df["severity"].value_counts())

    if actions.status_code == 200:
        act_df = pd.DataFrame(actions.json())
        if not act_df.empty:
            st.markdown("**Action Approval Status**")
            st.bar_chart(act_df["approval_status"].value_counts())

            st.markdown("**Action Execution Status**")
            st.bar_chart(act_df["execution_status"].value_counts())

    st.caption("Autonomous platform prototype: detect → decide → approve/execute → verify → audit")


scripts/start_backend.sh
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

cd "$(dirname "$0")/.."

if [ ! -d .venv ]; then
  python3 -m venv .venv
fi

source .venv/bin/activate
pip install --upgrade pip
pip install -r requirements.txt

set -a
source .env
set +a

exec uvicorn backend.main:app --host 0.0.0.0 --port 8000 --reload

#!/bin/bash
set -e

ROOT="$HOME/autonomous-platform"
echo "Creating project at $ROOT..."

rm -rf $ROOT
mkdir -p $ROOT
cd $ROOT

mkdir -p backend frontend scripts data

# --------------------------
# requirements.txt (cleaned)
# --------------------------
cat > requirements.txt <<'EOF'
fastapi
uvicorn
streamlit
requests
pandas
numpy
prometheus-client
PyJWT
python-multipart
EOF

# --------------------------
# .env (clean)
# --------------------------
cat > .env <<'EOF'
BACKEND_URL=http://127.0.0.1:8000
SESSION_SECRET=autonomous-secret
AUTO_APPROVE_LOW=true
ENABLE_REAL_REMEDIATION=false

CPU_THRESHOLD=85
ERROR_RATE_THRESHOLD=5
LATENCY_MS_THRESHOLD=1500
EOF

# --------------------------
# backend/main.py (FIXED + CLEAN)
# --------------------------
cat > backend/main.py <<'EOF'
from fastapi import FastAPI, Header, HTTPException
from fastapi.responses import Response
import os, time, jwt
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from prometheus_client import Counter, generate_latest

app = FastAPI()

SECRET = os.getenv("SESSION_SECRET","secret")

REQ = Counter("req_total","req",["path"])

USERS = {
    "admin":"admin",
    "operator":"operator",
    "reviewer":"reviewer"
}

def token(user, role):
    return jwt.encode({"sub":user,"role":role,"exp":datetime.utcnow()+timedelta(hours=4)}, SECRET, algorithm="HS256")

def validate(t):
    return jwt.decode(t, SECRET, algorithms=["HS256"])

@app.middleware("http")
async def m(req, call):
    res = await call(req)
    REQ.labels(req.url.path).inc()
    return res

@app.get("/metrics")
def metrics():
    return Response(generate_latest(), media_type="text/plain")

@app.get("/")
def home():
    return {"app":"autonomous platform"}

@app.get("/login/{user}")
def login(user:str):
    if user not in USERS:
        raise HTTPException(404)
    return {"token":token(user, USERS[user]), "role":USERS[user]}

@app.get("/health")
def health():
    return {"status":"ok"}

@app.post("/evaluate")
def evaluate(x_session_token:str=Header(...)):
    u = validate(x_session_token)
    return {
        "result": "Autonomous loop simulated",
        "actor": u["sub"]
    }
EOF

# --------------------------
# frontend/Home.py (clean)
# --------------------------
cat > frontend/Home.py <<'EOF'
import streamlit as st
import requests

backend="http://127.0.0.1:8000"

st.set_page_config(layout="wide")

st.title("Autonomous Platform")

user=st.selectbox("Login as",["admin","operator","reviewer"])

if st.button("Login"):
    r=requests.get(f"{backend}/login/{user}")
    st.session_state["token"]=r.json()["token"]
    st.success("Logged in")

if "token" in st.session_state:

    st.markdown("### Health")
    st.write(requests.get(f"{backend}/health").json())

    if st.button("Run Autonomous Loop"):
        r=requests.post(f"{backend}/evaluate",headers={"x-session-token":st.session_state["token"]})
        st.success(r.json())

    st.markdown("### Metrics")
    st.code(requests.get(f"{backend}/metrics").text[:1000])
EOF

# --------------------------
# scripts
# --------------------------
cat > scripts/start_backend.sh <<'EOF'
#!/bin/bash
python3 -m venv venv
source venv/bin/activate
pip install -r requirements.txt
uvicorn backend.main:app --reload
EOF

cat > scripts/start_frontend.sh <<'EOF'
#!/bin/bash
source venv/bin/activate
streamlit run frontend/Home.py
EOF

chmod +x scripts/*.sh

echo ""
echo "✅ PROJECT CREATED"
echo "Run:"
echo "bash scripts/start_backend.sh"
echo "bash scripts/start_frontend.sh"

One-shot bootstrap block
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -Eeuo pipefail

ROOT="$HOME/autonomous-platform-pro"
rm -rf "$ROOT"
mkdir -p "$ROOT"/{backend,frontend,scripts,k8s,argo,data,logs}
cd "$ROOT"

cat > requirements.txt <<'EOF'
fastapi
uvicorn
streamlit
requests
pandas
numpy
prometheus-client
PyJWT
python-multipart
SQLAlchemy
psycopg2-binary
redis
kafka-python
EOF

cat > .env <<'EOF'
ENV=production
BACKEND_URL=http://127.0.0.1:8000

SESSION_SECRET=autonomous-platform-secret
SESSION_EXP_MINUTES=480

AUTO_APPROVE_LOW=true
ENABLE_REAL_REMEDIATION=false
DEFAULT_NAMESPACE=default

CPU_THRESHOLD=85
ERROR_RATE_THRESHOLD=5

import json
import logging
import os
import subprocess
import time
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from typing import Dict, List, Optional

import jwt
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import redis
from fastapi import Depends, FastAPI, Header, HTTPException, Response
from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware
from kafka import KafkaProducer
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
from prometheus_client import Counter, Histogram, generate_latest
from sqlalchemy import Boolean, Column, Float, Integer, String, Text, create_engine
from sqlalchemy.orm import declarative_base, sessionmaker

APP_TITLE = "Autonomous Platform Pro"
APP_VERSION = "2.0.0"

SESSION_SECRET = os.getenv("SESSION_SECRET", "autonomous-platform-secret")
SESSION_EXP_MINUTES = int(os.getenv("SESSION_EXP_MINUTES", "480"))

AUTO_APPROVE_LOW = os.getenv("AUTO_APPROVE_LOW", "true").lower() == "true"
ENABLE_REAL_REMEDIATION = os.getenv("ENABLE_REAL_REMEDIATION", "false").lower() == "true"
DEFAULT_NAMESPACE = os.getenv("DEFAULT_NAMESPACE", "default")

CPU_THRESHOLD = float(os.getenv("CPU_THRESHOLD", "85"))
ERROR_RATE_THRESHOLD = float(os.getenv("ERROR_RATE_THRESHOLD", "5"))
LATENCY_MS_THRESHOLD = float(os.getenv("LATENCY_MS_THRESHOLD", "1500"))

DATABASE_URL = os.getenv("DATABASE_URL", "sqlite:///data/app.db")
REDIS_URL = os.getenv("REDIS_URL", "redis://127.0.0.1:6379/0")

KAFKA_ENABLED = os.getenv("KAFKA_ENABLED", "false").lower() == "true"
KAFKA_BOOTSTRAP_SERVERS = os.getenv("KAFKA_BOOTSTRAP_SERVERS", "127.0.0.1:9092")
KAFKA_TOPIC_TELEMETRY = os.getenv("KAFKA_TOPIC_TELEMETRY", "autonomous-telemetry")

ARGOCD_ENABLED = os.getenv("ARGOCD_ENABLED", "false").lower() == "true"
ARGOCD_SERVER = os.getenv("ARGOCD_SERVER", "http://127.0.0.1:8080")
ARGOCD_APP = os.getenv("ARGOCD_APP", "policy-compare")

STRUCTURED_LOG_FILE = os.getenv("STRUCTURED_LOG_FILE", "logs/app.jsonl")
os.makedirs("logs", exist_ok=True)
os.makedirs("data", exist_ok=True)

# --------------------------
# Structured logging
# --------------------------
logger = logging.getLogger("autonomous")
logger.setLevel(logging.INFO)
logger.handlers.clear()
fh = logging.FileHandler(STRUCTURED_LOG_FILE)
fh.setFormatter(logging.Formatter("%(message)s"))
logger.addHandler(fh)
sh = logging.StreamHandler()
sh.setFormatter(logging.Formatter("%(message)s"))
logger.addHandler(sh)

def jlog(event_type: str, actor: str, details: Dict):
    logger.info(json.dumps({
        "timestamp": datetime.utcnow().isoformat(),
        "event_type": event_type,
        "actor": actor,
        "details": details
    }, ensure_ascii=False))

# --------------------------
# Metrics
# --------------------------
REQUEST_COUNT = Counter("http_requests_total", "Total HTTP Requests", ["method", "endpoint", "status"])
REQUEST_LATENCY = Histogram("http_request_duration_seconds", "HTTP request latency", ["endpoint"])
AUTONOMOUS_EVALUATIONS = Counter("autonomous_evaluations_total", "Autonomous evaluation cycles")
INCIDENTS_RAISED = Counter("autonomous_incidents_total", "Incidents raised by severity", ["severity"])
ACTIONS_CREATED = Counter("autonomous_actions_total", "Actions created by type", ["action_type"])
ACTIONS_EXECUTED = Counter("autonomous_actions_executed_total", "Actions executed by result", ["result"])

# --------------------------
# Demo users / roles
# --------------------------
DEMO_USERS = {
    "admin": "admin",
    "reviewer": "compliance_reviewer",
    "operator": "operator",
    "viewer": "viewer",
}

# --------------------------
# Auth
# --------------------------
def create_token(user: str, role: str) -> str:
    payload = {
        "sub": user,
        "role": role,
        "exp": datetime.utcnow() + timedelta(minutes=SESSION_EXP_MINUTES),
    }
    return jwt.encode(payload, SESSION_SECRET, algorithm="HS256")

def decode_token(token: str) -> Dict:
    try:
        return jwt.decode(token, SESSION_SECRET, algorithms=["HS256"])
    except Exception:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Invalid or expired session token")

def require_session(x_session_token: Optional[str] = Header(default=None)) -> Dict:
    if not x_session_token:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Missing X-Session-Token")
    return decode_token(x_session_token)

def require_roles(session: Dict, roles: List[str]):
    if session["role"] not in roles:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="Access denied")

# --------------------------
# DB
# --------------------------
engine = create_engine(DATABASE_URL, future=True)
SessionLocal = sessionmaker(bind=engine, autoflush=False, autocommit=False, future=True)
Base = declarative_base()

class Telemetry(Base):
    __tablename__ = "telemetry"
    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True)
    service = Column(String(255), nullable=False)
    metric_name = Column(String(255), nullable=False)
    metric_value = Column(Float, nullable=False)
    ts = Column(String(64), nullable=False)

class Incident(Base):
    __tablename__ = "incidents"
    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True)
    service = Column(String(255), nullable=False)
    incident_type = Column(String(255), nullable=False)
    severity = Column(String(32), nullable=False)
    evidence_json = Column(Text, nullable=False)
    diagnosis = Column(Text, nullable=False)
    status = Column(String(32), nullable=False)
    created_at = Column(String(64), nullable=False)
    closed_at = Column(String(64), nullable=True)

class Action(Base):
    __tablename__ = "actions"
    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True)
    incident_id = Column(Integer, nullable=False)
    service = Column(String(255), nullable=False)
    action_type = Column(String(255), nullable=False)
    parameters_json = Column(Text, nullable=False)
    approval_required = Column(Boolean, nullable=False)
    approval_status = Column(String(32), nullable=False)
    execution_status = Column(String(32), nullable=False)
    created_at = Column(String(64), nullable=False)
    reviewed_by = Column(String(255), nullable=True)
    reviewed_at = Column(String(64), nullable=True)
    executed_by = Column(String(255), nullable=True)
    executed_at = Column(String(64), nullable=True)
    result_json = Column(Text, nullable=True)

Base.metadata.create_all(bind=engine)

# Redis / Kafka
# --------------------------
redis_client = None
try:
    redis_client = redis.from_url(REDIS_URL, decode_responses=True)
    redis_client.ping()
except Exception:
    redis_client = None

producer = None
if KAFKA_ENABLED:
    try:
        producer = KafkaProducer(
            bootstrap_servers=KAFKA_BOOTSTRAP_SERVERS,
            value_serializer=lambda v: json.dumps(v).encode("utf-8")
        )
    except Exception:
        producer = None

# --------------------------
# Models
# --------------------------
class TelemetryIn(BaseModel):
    service: str = Field(..., min_length=1)
    metric_name: str = Field(..., min_length=1)
    metric_value: float
    ts: Optional[str] = None

class BulkTelemetryIn(BaseModel):
    items: List[TelemetryIn]

class ApprovalIn(BaseModel):
    decision: str = Field(..., pattern="^(approved|rejected)$")
    comment: str = ""

class ExecuteIn(BaseModel):
    force: bool = False

# --------------------------
# Helpers
# --------------------------
def now_iso() -> str:
    return datetime.utcnow().isoformat()

def insert_telemetry(item: TelemetryIn):
    db = SessionLocal()
    db.add(Telemetry(
        service=item.service,
        metric_name=item.metric_name,
        metric_value=item.metric_value,
        ts=item.ts or now_iso()
    ))
    db.commit()
    db.close()

    if producer is not None:
        producer.send(KAFKA_TOPIC_TELEMETRY, item.model_dump())
        producer.flush()

def fetch_recent_telemetry(minutes: int = 240) -> pd.DataFrame:
    cutoff = datetime.utcnow() - timedelta(minutes=minutes)
    db = SessionLocal()
    rows = db.query(Telemetry).all()
    db.close()
    data = [{
        "id": r.id,
        "service": r.service,
        "metric_name": r.metric_name,
        "metric_value": r.metric_value,
        "ts": r.ts,
    } for r in rows if datetime.fromisoformat(r.ts) >= cutoff]
    return pd.DataFrame(data)

def unresolved_incident_exists(service: str, incident_type: str) -> bool:
    db = SessionLocal()
    row = db.query(Incident).filter(
        Incident.service == service,
        Incident.incident_type == incident_type,
        Incident.status.in_(["open", "under_review"])
    ).order_by(Incident.id.desc()).first()
    db.close()
    return row is not None

def create_incident(service: str, incident_type: str, severity: str, evidence: Dict, diagnosis: str) -> int:
    db = SessionLocal()
    obj = Incident(
        service=service,
        incident_type=incident_type,
        severity=severity,
        evidence_json=json.dumps(evidence),
        diagnosis=diagnosis,
        status="open",
        created_at=now_iso(),
        closed_at=None,
    )
    db.add(obj)
    db.commit()
    db.refresh(obj)
    db.close()
    INCIDENTS_RAISED.labels(severity=severity).inc()
    return obj.id

def create_action(incident_id: int, service: str, action_type: str, parameters: Dict, approval_required: bool) -> int:
    db = SessionLocal()
    obj = Action(
        incident_id=incident_id,
        service=service,
        action_type=action_type,
        parameters_json=json.dumps(parameters),
        approval_required=approval_required,
        approval_status="approved" if not approval_required else "pending",
        execution_status="ready" if not approval_required else "awaiting_approval",
        created_at=now_iso(),
    )
    db.add(obj)
    db.commit()
    db.refresh(obj)
    db.close()
    ACTIONS_CREATED.labels(action_type=action_type).inc()
    return obj.id

def list_incidents() -> List[Dict]:
    db = SessionLocal()
    rows = db.query(Incident).order_by(Incident.id.desc()).all()
    db.close()
    return [{
        "id": r.id,
        "service": r.service,
        "incident_type": r.incident_type,
        "severity": r.severity,
        "evidence": json.loads(r.evidence_json),
        "diagnosis": r.diagnosis,
        "status": r.status,
        "created_at": r.created_at,
        "closed_at": r.closed_at,
    } for r in rows]

def list_actions() -> List[Dict]:
    db = SessionLocal()
    rows = db.query(Action).order_by(Action.id.desc()).all()
    db.close()
    return [{
        "id": r.id,
        "incident_id": r.incident_id,
        "service": r.service,
        "action_type": r.action_type,
        "parameters": json.loads(r.parameters_json),
        "approval_required": r.approval_required,
        "approval_status": r.approval_status,
        "execution_status": r.execution_status,
        "created_at": r.created_at,
        "reviewed_by": r.reviewed_by,
        "reviewed_at": r.reviewed_at,
        "executed_by": r.executed_by,
        "executed_at": r.executed_at,
        "result": json.loads(r.result_json) if r.result_json else {},
    } for r in rows]

def update_action_approval(action_id: int, reviewer: str, decision: str, comment: str):
    db = SessionLocal()
    row = db.query(Action).filter(Action.id == action_id).first()
    if not row:
      db.close()
      raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Action not found")
    row.approval_status = decision
    row.execution_status = "ready" if decision == "approved" else "cancelled"
    row.reviewed_by = reviewer
    row.reviewed_at = now_iso()
    row.result_json = json.dumps({"review_comment": comment})
    db.commit()
    db.close()

def update_action_execution(action_id: int, executor: str, status: str, result: Dict):
    db = SessionLocal()
    row = db.query(Action).filter(Action.id == action_id).first()
    if not row:
      db.close()
      raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Action not found")
    row.execution_status = status
    row.executed_by = executor
    row.executed_at = now_iso()
    row.result_json = json.dumps(result)
    db.commit()
    db.close()

def close_incident(incident_id: int):
    db = SessionLocal()
    row = db.query(Incident).filter(Incident.id == incident_id).first()
    if row:
        row.status = "closed"
        row.closed_at = now_iso()
        db.commit()
    db.close()

def rolling_zscore(values: List[float]) -> float:
    arr = np.array(values, dtype=float)
    if len(arr) < 3:
        return 0.0
    baseline = arr[:-1]
    std = np.std(baseline)
    if std == 0:
        return 0.0
    return float((arr[-1] - np.mean(baseline)) / (std + 1e-9))

def classify_service(service_df: pd.DataFrame) -> List[Dict]:
    service = service_df["service"].iloc[0]
    incidents = []

    cpu_series = service_df[service_df["metric_name"] == "cpu_percent"]["metric_value"].tolist()
    err_series = service_df[service_df["metric_name"] == "error_rate"]["metric_value"].tolist()
    lat_series = service_df[service_df["metric_name"] == "latency_ms"]["metric_value"].tolist()

    cpu = cpu_series[-1] if cpu_series else None
    err = err_series[-1] if err_series else None
    lat = lat_series[-1] if lat_series else None

    cpu_z = rolling_zscore(cpu_series) if cpu_series else 0.0
    err_z = rolling_zscore(err_series) if err_series else 0.0
    lat_z = rolling_zscore(lat_series) if lat_series else 0.0

    if cpu is not None and (cpu >= CPU_THRESHOLD or cpu_z >= 2.5):
        incidents.append({
            "service": service,
            "incident_type": "cpu_saturation",
            "severity": "low" if cpu < 95 else "medium",
            "evidence": {"cpu_percent": cpu, "cpu_zscore": round(cpu_z, 2), "threshold": CPU_THRESHOLD},
            "diagnosis": f"CPU saturation detected for {service}",
        })

    if err is not None and (err >= ERROR_RATE_THRESHOLD or err_z >= 2.5):
        incidents.append({
            "service": service,
            "incident_type": "error_rate_spike",
            "severity": "medium" if err < 10 else "high",
            "evidence": {"error_rate": err, "error_zscore": round(err_z, 2), "threshold": ERROR_RATE_THRESHOLD},
            "diagnosis": f"Error-rate spike detected for {service}",
        })

    if lat is not None and (lat >= LATENCY_MS_THRESHOLD or lat_z >= 2.5):
        incidents.append({
            "service": service,
            "incident_type": "latency_degradation",
            "severity": "low" if lat < 2500 else "medium",
            "evidence": {"latency_ms": lat, "latency_zscore": round(lat_z, 2), "threshold": LATENCY_MS_THRESHOLD},
            "diagnosis": f"Latency degradation detected for {service}",
        })

    if err is not None and lat is not None and err >= ERROR_RATE_THRESHOLD and lat >= LATENCY_MS_THRESHOLD:
        incidents.append({
            "service": service,
            "incident_type": "release_regression_suspected",
            "severity": "high",
            "evidence": {"error_rate": err, "latency_ms": lat},
            "diagnosis": f"Combined error-rate and latency regression suspected for {service}",
        })

    return incidents

def plan_action(incident: Dict) -> Dict:
    incident_type = incident["incident_type"]
    service = incident["service"]
    severity = incident["severity"]

    approval_required = severity in ["medium", "high"]
    if severity == "low" and AUTO_APPROVE_LOW:
        approval_required = False

    if incident_type in ["cpu_saturation", "latency_degradation"]:
        return {
            "service": service,
            "action_type": "scale_up",
            "parameters": {"deployment": service, "replicas_delta": 1, "namespace": DEFAULT_NAMESPACE},
            "approval_required": approval_required,
        }

    if incident_type == "error_rate_spike":
        return {
            "service": service,
            "action_type": "restart_service",
            "parameters": {"deployment": service, "namespace": DEFAULT_NAMESPACE},
            "approval_required": approval_required,
        }

    if incident_type == "release_regression_suspected":
        return {
            "service": service,
            "action_type": "rollback_release",
            "parameters": {"deployment": service, "namespace": DEFAULT_NAMESPACE},
            "approval_required": True,
        }

    return {
        "service": service,
        "action_type": "observe_only",
        "parameters": {"deployment": service, "namespace": DEFAULT_NAMESPACE},
        "approval_required": True,
    }

def run_command(cmd: List[str]) -> Dict:
    try:
        proc = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, check=True)
        return {"success": True, "stdout": proc.stdout, "stderr": proc.stderr, "cmd": cmd}
    except subprocess.CalledProcessError as e:
        return {"success": False, "stdout": e.stdout, "stderr": e.stderr, "cmd": cmd}

def execute_remediation(action: Dict) -> Dict:
    action_type = action["action_type"]
    params = action["parameters"]
    deployment = params.get("deployment", "")
    namespace = params.get("namespace", DEFAULT_NAMESPACE)

    if not ENABLE_REAL_REMEDIATION:
        return {
            "mode": "dry_run",
            "success": True,
            "message": f"Simulated {action_type} for {deployment}",
            "parameters": params,
        }

    if action_type == "restart_service":
        return run_command(["kubectl", "rollout", "restart", f"deployment/{deployment}", "-n", namespace])

    if action_type == "rollback_release":
        return run_command(["kubectl", "rollout", "undo", f"deployment/{deployment}", "-n", namespace])

    if action_type == "scale_up":
        current = run_command(["kubectl", "get", f"deployment/{deployment}", "-n", namespace, "-o", "jsonpath={.spec.replicas}"])
        if not current["success"]:
            return current
        desired = int(current["stdout"] or "1") + int(params.get("replicas_delta", 1))
        return run_command(["kubectl", "scale", f"deployment/{deployment}", f"--replicas={desired}", "-n", namespace])

    return {"mode": "noop", "success": False, "message": f"Unsupported action {action_type}"}

def enqueue_action(action_id: int):
    if redis_client is None:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail="Redis not available")
    redis_client.rpush("autonomous:actions", json.dumps({"action_id": action_id}))

def process_one_queue_item(actor: str = "worker") -> Dict:
    if redis_client is None:
        return {"status": "redis_unavailable"}
    raw = redis_client.lpop("autonomous:actions")
    if not raw:
        return {"status": "empty"}
    payload = json.loads(raw)
    action_id = int(payload["action_id"])
    actions = [a for a in list_actions() if a["id"] == action_id]
    if not actions:
        return {"status": "missing_action", "action_id": action_id}
    action = actions[0]
    if action["approval_required"] and action["approval_status"] != "approved":
        return {"status": "not_approved", "action_id": action_id}
    result = execute_remediation(action)
    status = "executed" if result.get("success") else "failed"
    update_action_execution(action_id, actor, status, result)
    ACTIONS_EXECUTED.labels(result=status).inc()
    if status == "executed":
        close_incident(action["incident_id"])
    jlog("action_processed_from_queue", actor, {"action_id": action_id, "status": status})
    return {"status": status, "action_id": action_id, "result": result}

# --------------------------
# FastAPI
# --------------------------
app = FastAPI(title=APP_TITLE, version=APP_VERSION)
app.add_middleware(
    CORSMiddleware,
    allow_origins=["*"],
    allow_credentials=True,
    allow_methods=["*"],
    allow_headers=["*"],
)

@app.middleware("http")
async def metrics_middleware(request, call_next):
    start = time.time()
    response = await call_next(request)
    duration = time.time() - start
    REQUEST_COUNT.labels(method=request.method, endpoint=request.url.path, status=response.status_code).inc()
    REQUEST_LATENCY.labels(endpoint=request.url.path).observe(duration)
    return response

@app.get("/auth/login/{user}")
def login(user: str):
    role = DEMO_USERS.get(user)
    if not role:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="User not found")
    token = create_token(user, role)
    jlog("login", user, {"role": role})
    return {"token": token, "user": user, "role": role}

@app.get("/auth/me")
def me(session: Dict = Depends(require_session)):
    return {"user": session["sub"], "role": session["role"]}

@app.get("/health")
def health():
    return {
        "status": "ok",
        "version": APP_VERSION,
        "mode": "autonomous_enterprise",
        "real_remediation_enabled": ENABLE_REAL_REMEDIATION,
        "redis_available": redis_client is not None,
        "kafka_enabled": KAFKA_ENABLED and producer is not None,
        "database_url": DATABASE_URL,
    }

@app.get("/metrics")
def metrics():
    return Response(generate_latest(), media_type="text/plain")

@app.post("/telemetry")
def ingest_telemetry(item: TelemetryIn, session: Dict = Depends(require_session)):
    require_roles(session, ["admin", "operator", "compliance_reviewer"])
    insert_telemetry(item)
    jlog("telemetry_ingest", session["sub"], item.model_dump())
    return {"status": "ingested"}

@app.post("/telemetry/bulk")
def ingest_bulk(payload: BulkTelemetryIn, session: Dict = Depends(require_session)):
    require_roles(session, ["admin", "operator", "compliance_reviewer"])
    for item in payload.items:
        insert_telemetry(item)
    jlog("telemetry_bulk_ingest", session["sub"], {"count": len(payload.items)})
    return {"status": "ingested", "count": len(payload.items)}

@app.post("/telemetry/sample")
def telemetry_sample(session: Dict = Depends(require_session)):
    require_roles(session, ["admin", "operator"])
    rows = [
        TelemetryIn(service="orders-api", metric_name="cpu_percent", metric_value=91),
        TelemetryIn(service="orders-api", metric_name="error_rate", metric_value=7),
        TelemetryIn(service="orders-api", metric_name="latency_ms", metric_value=2200),
        TelemetryIn(service="billing-worker", metric_name="cpu_percent", metric_value=62),
        TelemetryIn(service="billing-worker", metric_name="error_rate", metric_value=1),
        TelemetryIn(service="billing-worker", metric_name="latency_ms", metric_value=420),
        TelemetryIn(service="claims-service", metric_name="cpu_percent", metric_value=87),
        TelemetryIn(service="claims-service", metric_name="error_rate", metric_value=0.5),
        TelemetryIn(service="claims-service", metric_name="latency_ms", metric_value=1600),
    ]
    for row in rows:
        insert_telemetry(row)
    jlog("telemetry_sample_generated", session["sub"], {"count": len(rows)})
    return {"status": "generated", "count": len(rows)}

@app.get("/telemetry/recent")
def telemetry_recent(session: Dict = Depends(require_session)):
    df = fetch_recent_telemetry()
    return df.to_dict(orient="records")

@app.post("/autonomous/evaluate")
def autonomous_evaluate(session: Dict = Depends(require_session)):
    require_roles(session, ["admin", "operator", "compliance_reviewer"])
    AUTONOMOUS_EVALUATIONS.inc()

    df = fetch_recent_telemetry()
    if df.empty:
        return {"status": "no_data", "created_incidents": 0, "created_actions": 0}

    created_incidents = 0
    created_actions = 0

    for service, service_df in df.groupby("service"):
        incidents = classify_service(service_df)
        for incident in incidents:
            if unresolved_incident_exists(service, incident["incident_type"]):
                continue
            incident_id = create_incident(
                incident["service"],
                incident["incident_type"],
                incident["severity"],
                incident["evidence"],
                incident["diagnosis"],
            )
            created_incidents += 1

            planned = plan_action(incident)
            create_action(
                incident_id=incident_id,
                service=planned["service"],
                action_type=planned["action_type"],
                parameters=planned["parameters"],
                approval_required=planned["approval_required"],
            )
            created_actions += 1

            jlog("incident_created", session["sub"], {
                "incident_id": incident_id,
                "service": incident["service"],
                "incident_type": incident["incident_type"],
                "severity": incident["severity"],
            })

    return {"status": "evaluated", "created_incidents": created_incidents, "created_actions": created_actions}

@app.get("/incidents")
def incidents(session: Dict = Depends(require_session)):
    return list_incidents()

@app.get("/actions")
def actions(session: Dict = Depends(require_session)):
    return list_actions()

@app.post("/actions/{action_id}/approve")
def action_approve(action_id: int, payload: ApprovalIn, session: Dict = Depends(require_session)):
    require_roles(session, ["admin", "compliance_reviewer"])
    update_action_approval(action_id, session["sub"], payload.decision, payload.comment)
    jlog("action_reviewed", session["sub"], {"action_id": action_id, "decision": payload.decision, "comment": payload.comment})
    return {"status": payload.decision}

@app.post("/actions/{action_id}/execute")
def action_execute(action_id: int, payload: ExecuteIn, session: Dict = Depends(require_session)):
    require_roles(session, ["admin", "operator", "compliance_reviewer"])
    target = next((a for a in list_actions() if a["id"] == action_id), None)
    if not target:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Action not found")
    if target["approval_required"] and target["approval_status"] != "approved" and not payload.force:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Action requires approval")
    result = execute_remediation(target)
    status = "executed" if result.get("success") else "failed"
    update_action_execution(action_id, session["sub"], status, result)
    ACTIONS_EXECUTED.labels(result=status).inc()
    if status == "executed":
        close_incident(target["incident_id"])
    jlog("action_executed", session["sub"], {"action_id": action_id, "status": status, "result": result})
    return {"status": status, "result": result}

@app.post("/actions/{action_id}/enqueue")
def action_enqueue(action_id: int, session: Dict = Depends(require_session)):
    require_roles(session, ["admin", "operator", "compliance_reviewer"])
    enqueue_action(action_id)
    jlog("action_enqueued", session["sub"], {"action_id": action_id})
    return {"status": "queued", "action_id": action_id}

@app.post("/worker/process-once")
def worker_process_once(session: Dict = Depends(require_session)):
    require_roles(session, ["admin", "operator"])
    return process_one_queue_item(session["sub"])

@app.get("/dashboard/summary")
def dashboard_summary(session: Dict = Depends(require_session)):
    inc = list_incidents()
    act = list_actions()
    tele = fetch_recent_telemetry()
    return {
        "total_incidents": len(inc),
        "open_incidents": sum(1 for i in inc if i["status"] in ["open", "under_review"]),
        "high_incidents": sum(1 for i in inc if i["severity"] == "high"),
        "medium_incidents": sum(1 for i in inc if i["severity"] == "medium"),
        "low_incidents": sum(1 for i in inc if i["severity"] == "low"),
        "total_actions": len(act),
        "approved_actions": sum(1 for a in act if a["approval_status"] == "approved"),
        "pending_actions": sum(1 for a in act if a["approval_status"] == "pending"),
        "executed_actions": sum(1 for a in act if a["execution_status"] == "executed"),
        "failed_actions": sum(1 for a in act if a["execution_status"] == "failed"),
        "services_monitored": int(tele["service"].nunique()) if not tele.empty else 0,
    }

@app.get("/dashboard/charts")
def dashboard_charts(session: Dict = Depends(require_session)):
    df = fetch_recent_telemetry()
    if df.empty:
        return {"cpu_percent": [], "error_rate": [], "latency_ms": []}
    df["ts"] = pd.to_datetime(df["ts"])
    out = {}
    for metric in ["cpu_percent", "error_rate", "latency_ms"]:
        out[metric] = df[df["metric_name"] == metric][["ts", "service", "metric_value"]].to_dict(orient="records")
    return out

@app.post("/gitops/argocd/sync")
def gitops_argocd_sync(session: Dict = Depends(require_session)):
    require_roles(session, ["admin", "operator"])
    if not ARGOCD_ENABLED:
        return {"status": "dry_run", "message": f"Would sync ArgoCD app {ARGOCD_APP} on {ARGOCD_SERVER}"}
    try:
        proc = subprocess.run(["argocd", "app", "sync", ARGOCD_APP, "--server", ARGOCD_SERVER], capture_output=True, text=True, check=True)
        jlog("argocd_sync", session["sub"], {"app": ARGOCD_APP, "stdout": proc.stdout})
        return {"status": "executed", "stdout": proc.stdout, "stderr": proc.stderr}
    except subprocess.CalledProcessError as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail={"stdout": e.stdout, "stderr": e.stderr})
EOF

cat > frontend/Home.py <<'EOF'
import json
import os
import pandas as pd
import requests
import streamlit as st

BACKEND_URL = os.getenv("BACKEND_URL", "http://127.0.0.1:8000")

def api_get(path, token="", params=None, timeout=60):
    headers = {"X-Session-Token": token} if token else {}
    return requests.get(f"{BACKEND_URL}{path}", headers=headers, params=params or {}, timeout=timeout)

def api_post(path, token="", body=None, timeout=120):
    headers = {"X-Session-Token": token} if token else {}
    return requests.post(f"{BACKEND_URL}{path}", headers=headers, json=body or {}, timeout=timeout)

st.set_page_config(page_title="Autonomous Platform Pro", layout="wide")
st.title("Autonomous Platform Pro")
st.caption("Prometheus • JWT RBAC • JSON logs • anomaly detection • approval workflow • Redis queue • Kafka telemetry • ArgoCD sync")

if "token" not in st.session_state:
    st.session_state["token"] = ""
if "user" not in st.session_state:
    st.session_state["user"] = ""
if "role" not in st.session_state:
    st.session_state["role"] = ""

with st.sidebar:
    user = st.selectbox("Login", ["admin", "reviewer", "operator", "viewer"])
    if st.button("Login"):
        r = requests.get(f"{BACKEND_URL}/auth/login/{user}", timeout=30)
        if r.status_code == 200:
            data = r.json()
            st.session_state["token"] = data["token"]
            st.session_state["user"] = data["user"]
            st.session_state["role"] = data["role"]
        else:
            st.error(r.text)

    if st.session_state["token"]:
        st.success(f"{st.session_state['user']} ({st.session_state['role']})")

token = st.session_state["token"]
if not token:
    st.stop()

tabs = st.tabs(["Overview", "Telemetry", "Incidents & Actions", "Boardroom", "GitOps"])

with tabs[0]:
    health = api_get("/health", token=token)
    if health.status_code == 200:
        st.json(health.json())

    summary = api_get("/dashboard/summary", token=token)
    if summary.status_code == 200:
        s = summary.json()
        c1, c2, c3, c4 = st.columns(4)
        c1.metric("Open Incidents", s["open_incidents"])
        c2.metric("High Incidents", s["high_incidents"])
        c3.metric("Pending Actions", s["pending_actions"])
        c4.metric("Executed Actions", s["executed_actions"])

    a1, a2 = st.columns(2)
    with a1:
        if st.button("Generate Sample Telemetry", use_container_width=True):
            r = api_post("/telemetry/sample", token=token)
            st.write(r.json())
    with a2:
        if st.button("Run Autonomous Evaluation", use_container_width=True):
            r = api_post("/autonomous/evaluate", token=token)
            st.write(r.json())

with tabsst.subheader("Manual Telemetry")
    c1, c2, c3 = st.columns(3)
    with c1:
        service = st.text_input("Service", value="orders-api")
    with c2:
        metric_name = st.selectbox("Metric", ["cpu_percent", "error_rate", "latency_ms"])
    with c3:
        metric_value = st.number_input("Metric value", value=90.0)

    if st.button("Send Telemetry", use_container_width=True):
        r = api_post("/telemetry", token=token, body={
            "service": service,
            "metric_name": metric_name,
            "metric_value": metric_value
        })
        st.write(r.json())

    st.markdown("---")
    recent = api_get("/telemetry/recent", token=token)
    if recent.status_code == 200:
        df = pd.DataFrame(recent.json())
        st.dataframe(df, use_container_width=True, hide_index=True)

with tabs[2]:
    inc = api_get("/incidents", token=token)
    act = api_get("/actions", token=token)

    st.subheader("Incidents")
    if inc.status_code == 200:
        inc_df = pd.DataFrame(inc.json())
        st.dataframe(inc_df, use_container_width=True, hide_index=True)

    st.subheader("Actions")
    if act.status_code == 200:
        act_df = pd.DataFrame(act.json())
        st.dataframe(act_df, use_container_width=True, hide_index=True)

        if not act_df.empty:
            selected = st.selectbox("Select action ID", act_df["id"].tolist())
            r1, r2, r3, r4 = st.columns(4)
            with r1:
                if st.button("Approve", use_container_width=True):
                    st.write(api_post(f"/actions/{selected}/approve", token=token, body={"decision": "approved", "comment": "Approved from UI"}).json())
            with r2:
                if st.button("Reject", use_container_width=True):
                    st.write(api_post(f"/actions/{selected}/approve", token=token, body={"decision": "rejected", "comment": "Rejected from UI"}).json())
            with r3:
                if st.button("Execute", use_container_width=True):
                    st.write(api_post(f"/actions/{selected}/execute", token=token, body={"force": False}).json())
            with r4:
                if st.button("Queue", use_container_width=True):
                    st.write(api_post(f"/actions/{selected}/enqueue", token=token).json())

with tabs[3]:
    summary = api_get("/dashboard/summary", token=token)
    charts = api_get("/dashboard/charts", token=token)
    if summary.status_code == 200:
        s = summary.json()
        m1, m2, m3, m4 = st.columns(4)
        m1.metric("Services", s["services_monitored"])
        m2.metric("Incidents", s["total_incidents"])
        m3.metric("Actions", s["total_actions"])
        m4.metric("Approved", s["approved_actions"])

    if charts.status_code == 200:
        chart_payload = charts.json()
        for metric in ["cpu_percent", "error_rate", "latency_ms"]:
            df = pd.DataFrame(chart_payload.get(metric, []))
            if not df.empty:
                df["ts"] = pd.to_datetime(df["ts"])
                st.markdown(f"**{metric}**")
                st.line_chart(df.pivot(index="ts", columns="service", values="metric_value"))

with tabs[4]:
    if st.button("Sync ArgoCD App", use_container_width=True):
        st.write(api_post("/gitops/argocd/sync", token=token).json())

    if st.button("Process One Queue Item", use_container_width=True):
        st.write(api_post("/worker/process-once", token=token).json())
EOF

cat > scripts/worker_loop.sh <<'EOF'
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -Eeuo pipefail
cd "$(dirname "$0")/.."
source .venv/bin/activate
set -a
source .env
set +a

TOKEN=$(python - <<'PY'
import os, requests
r = requests.get("http://127.0.0.1:8000/auth/login/operator", timeout=30)
print(r.json()["token"])
PY
)

while true; do
  curl -s -X POST "http://127.0.0.1:8000/worker/process-once" -H "X-Session-Token: $TOKEN" -H "Content-Type: application/json" -d '{}' || true
  sleep 5
done
EOF

5 final slides
Slide 1 — Problem

Modern enterprise operations are overloaded by alerts, incidents, and manual triage.
Static monitoring catches symptoms late and scales poorly.
Delayed action increases downtime, compliance risk, and support cost.

Slide 2 — Solution

An autonomous operations platform ingests telemetry, detects anomalies, raises incidents, and recommends bounded corrective actions.
Low-risk actions can auto-approve; medium/high risk actions follow human approval.
Every decision is logged, measurable, and exportable.

Slide 3 — Security & Governance

JWT role-based access controls separate admin, reviewer, operator, and viewer scopes.
Structured JSON logs create a full audit trail.
Approval workflow ensures human control over sensitive remediation.

Slide 4 — Platform Capabilities

Prometheus /metrics for observability.
Redis-backed action queue for asynchronous execution.
Kafka telemetry forwarding for streaming integration.
PostgreSQL or SQLite storage.
Kubernetes remediation hooks and ArgoCD sync integration.

Slide 5 — Business Impact

Faster incident detection and response.
Lower manual toil and reduced operational cost.
Higher enterprise trust through explainability, approvals, and governance.
Ready to evolve from prototype to production operations control plane.


30-second sales pitch
“Today’s enterprise systems generate more alerts and operational noise than teams can reliably handle manually.
Our autonomous platform closes that gap by ingesting telemetry, detecting anomalies, creating incidents, and driving policy-bound remediation with clear approvals and audit trails.
It combines observability, anomaly detection, secure role-based control, queue-driven execution, and GitOps hooks into one operational control plane.
The result is faster recovery, less manual toil, lower risk, and a direct path from prototype to enterprise-grade autonomous operations.”
