cat > backend/main.py <<'EOF'
import json
import logging
import os
import subprocess
import time
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from typing import Dict, List, Optional

import jwt
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import redis
from fastapi import Depends, FastAPI, Header, HTTPException, Response
from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware
from kafka import KafkaProducer
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
from prometheus_client import Counter, Histogram, generate_latest
from sqlalchemy import Boolean, Column, Float, Integer, String, Text, create_engine
from sqlalchemy.orm import declarative_base, sessionmaker

APP_TITLE = "Autonomous Platform Pro"
APP_VERSION = "2.1.0"

SESSION_SECRET = os.getenv("SESSION_SECRET", "autonomous-platform-secret")
SESSION_EXP_MINUTES = int(os.getenv("SESSION_EXP_MINUTES", "480"))

AUTO_APPROVE_LOW = os.getenv("AUTO_APPROVE_LOW", "true").lower() == "true"
ENABLE_REAL_REMEDIATION = os.getenv("ENABLE_REAL_REMEDIATION", "false").lower() == "true"
DEFAULT_NAMESPACE = os.getenv("DEFAULT_NAMESPACE", "default")

CPU_THRESHOLD = float(os.getenv("CPU_THRESHOLD", "85"))
ERROR_RATE_THRESHOLD = float(os.getenv("ERROR_RATE_THRESHOLD", "5"))
LATENCY_MS_THRESHOLD = float(os.getenv("LATENCY_MS_THRESHOLD", "1500"))

DATABASE_URL = os.getenv("DATABASE_URL", "postgresql://autonomous:autonomous@postgres:5432/autonomous")
REDIS_URL = os.getenv("REDIS_URL", "redis://redis:6379/0")

KAFKA_ENABLED = os.getenv("KAFKA_ENABLED", "true").lower() == "true"
KAFKA_BOOTSTRAP_SERVERS = os.getenv("KAFKA_BOOTSTRAP_SERVERS", "kafka:9092")
KAFKA_TOPIC_TELEMETRY = os.getenv("KAFKA_TOPIC_TELEMETRY", "autonomous-telemetry")

ARGOCD_ENABLED = os.getenv("ARGOCD_ENABLED", "false").lower() == "true"
ARGOCD_SERVER = os.getenv("ARGOCD_SERVER", "http://argocd-server:8080")
ARGOCD_APP = os.getenv("ARGOCD_APP", "policy-compare")

STRUCTURED_LOG_FILE = os.getenv("STRUCTURED_LOG_FILE", "logs/app.jsonl")
os.makedirs("logs", exist_ok=True)

# --------------------------
# Logging
# --------------------------
logger = logging.getLogger("autonomous")
logger.setLevel(logging.INFO)
logger.handlers.clear()
fh = logging.FileHandler(STRUCTURED_LOG_FILE)
fh.setFormatter(logging.Formatter("%(message)s"))
logger.addHandler(fh)
sh = logging.StreamHandler()
sh.setFormatter(logging.Formatter("%(message)s"))
logger.addHandler(sh)

def jlog(event_type: str, actor: str, details: Dict):
    payload = {
        "timestamp": datetime.utcnow().isoformat(),
        "event_type": event_type,
        "actor": actor,
        "details": details,
    }
    logger.info(json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False))

# --------------------------
# Metrics
# --------------------------
REQUEST_COUNT = Counter("http_requests_total", "Total HTTP Requests", ["method", "endpoint", "status"])
REQUEST_LATENCY = Histogram("http_request_duration_seconds", "HTTP request latency", ["endpoint"])
AUTONOMOUS_EVALUATIONS = Counter("autonomous_evaluations_total", "Autonomous evaluation cycles")
INCIDENTS_RAISED = Counter("autonomous_incidents_total", "Incidents raised by severity", ["severity"])
ACTIONS_CREATED = Counter("autonomous_actions_total", "Actions created by type", ["action_type"])
ACTIONS_EXECUTED = Counter("autonomous_actions_executed_total", "Actions executed by result", ["result"])

# --------------------------
# Demo users / roles
# --------------------------
DEMO_USERS = {
    "admin": "admin",
    "reviewer": "compliance_reviewer",
    "operator": "operator",
    "viewer": "viewer",
}

# --------------------------
# Auth
# --------------------------
def create_token(user: str, role: str) -> str:
    payload = {
        "sub": user,
        "role": role,
        "exp": datetime.utcnow() + timedelta(minutes=SESSION_EXP_MINUTES),
    }
    return jwt.encode(payload, SESSION_SECRET, algorithm="HS256")

def decode_token(token: str) -> Dict:
    try:
        return jwt.decode(token, SESSION_SECRET, algorithms=["HS256"])
    except Exception:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Invalid or expired session token")

def require_session(x_session_token: Optional[str] = Header(default=None)) -> Dict:
    if not x_session_token:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Missing X-Session-Token")
    return decode_token(x_session_token)

def require_roles(session: Dict, roles: List[str]):
    if session["role"] not in roles:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="Access denied")

# --------------------------
# DB
# --------------------------
engine = create_engine(DATABASE_URL, future=True, pool_pre_ping=True)
SessionLocal = sessionmaker(bind=engine, autoflush=False, autocommit=False, future=True)
Base = declarative_base()

class Telemetry(Base):
    __tablename__ = "telemetry"
    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True)
    service = Column(String(255), nullable=False)
    metric_name = Column(String(255), nullable=False)
    metric_value = Column(Float, nullable=False)
    ts = Column(String(64), nullable=False)

class Incident(Base):
    __tablename__ = "incidents"
    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True)
    service = Column(String(255), nullable=False)
    incident_type = Column(String(255), nullable=False)
    severity = Column(String(32), nullable=False)
    evidence_json = Column(Text, nullable=False)
    diagnosis = Column(Text, nullable=False)
    status = Column(String(32), nullable=False)
    created_at = Column(String(64), nullable=False)
    closed_at = Column(String(64), nullable=True)

class Action(Base):
    __tablename__ = "actions"
    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True)
    incident_id = Column(Integer, nullable=False)
    service = Column(String(255), nullable=False)
    action_type = Column(String(255), nullable=False)
    parameters_json = Column(Text, nullable=False)
    approval_required = Column(Boolean, nullable=False)
    approval_status = Column(String(32), nullable=False)
    execution_status = Column(String(32), nullable=False)
    created_at = Column(String(64), nullable=False)
    reviewed_by = Column(String(255), nullable=True)
    reviewed_at = Column(String(64), nullable=True)
    executed_by = Column(String(255), nullable=True)
    executed_at = Column(String(64), nullable=True)
    result_json = Column(Text, nullable=True)

Base.metadata.create_all(bind=engine)

# --------------------------
# Redis / Kafka
# --------------------------
redis_client = None
try:
    redis_client = redis.from_url(REDIS_URL, decode_responses=True)
    redis_client.ping()
except Exception:
    redis_client = None

producer = None
if KAFKA_ENABLED:
    try:
        producer = KafkaProducer(
            bootstrap_servers=KAFKA_BOOTSTRAP_SERVERS,
            value_serializer=lambda v: json.dumps(v).encode("utf-8")
        )
    except Exception:
        producer = None

# --------------------------
# Models
# --------------------------
class TelemetryIn(BaseModel):
    service: str = Field(..., min_length=1)
    metric_name: str = Field(..., min_length=1)
    metric_value: float
    ts: Optional[str] = None

class BulkTelemetryIn(BaseModel):
    items: List[TelemetryIn]

class ApprovalIn(BaseModel):
    decision: str = Field(..., pattern="^(approved|rejected)$")
    comment: str = ""

class ExecuteIn(BaseModel):
    force: bool = False

# --------------------------
# Helpers
# --------------------------
def now_iso() -> str:
    return datetime.utcnow().isoformat()

def insert_telemetry(item: TelemetryIn):
    db = SessionLocal()
    db.add(Telemetry(
        service=item.service,
        metric_name=item.metric_name,
        metric_value=item.metric_value,
        ts=item.ts or now_iso(),
    ))
    db.commit()
    db.close()

    if producer is not None:
        producer.send(KAFKA_TOPIC_TELEMETRY, item.model_dump())
        producer.flush()

def fetch_recent_telemetry(minutes: int = 240) -> pd.DataFrame:
    cutoff = datetime.utcnow() - timedelta(minutes=minutes)
    db = SessionLocal()
    rows = db.query(Telemetry).all()
    db.close()
    data = [{
        "id": r.id,
        "service": r.service,
        "metric_name": r.metric_name,
        "metric_value": r.metric_value,
        "ts": r.ts,
    } for r in rows if datetime.fromisoformat(r.ts) >= cutoff]
    return pd.DataFrame(data)

def unresolved_incident_exists(service: str, incident_type: str) -> bool:
    db = SessionLocal()
    row = db.query(Incident).filter(
        Incident.service == service,
        Incident.incident_type == incident_type,
        Incident.status.in_(["open", "under_review"])
    ).order_by(Incident.id.desc()).first()
    db.close()
    return row is not None

def create_incident(service: str, incident_type: str, severity: str, evidence: Dict, diagnosis: str) -> int:
    db = SessionLocal()
    obj = Incident(
        service=service,
        incident_type=incident_type,
        severity=severity,
        evidence_json=json.dumps(evidence),
        diagnosis=diagnosis,
        status="open",
        created_at=now_iso(),
        closed_at=None,
    )
    db.add(obj)
    db.commit()
    db.refresh(obj)
    db.close()
    INCIDENTS_RAISED.labels(severity=severity).inc()
    return obj.id

def create_action(incident_id: int, service: str, action_type: str, parameters: Dict, approval_required: bool) -> int:
    db = SessionLocal()
    obj = Action(
        incident_id=incident_id,
        service=service,
        action_type=action_type,
        parameters_json=json.dumps(parameters),
        approval_required=approval_required,
        approval_status="approved" if not approval_required else "pending",
        execution_status="ready" if not approval_required else "awaiting_approval",
        created_at=now_iso(),
    )
    db.add(obj)
    db.commit()
    db.refresh(obj)
    db.close()
    ACTIONS_CREATED.labels(action_type=action_type).inc()
    return obj.id

def list_incidents() -> List[Dict]:
    db = SessionLocal()
    rows = db.query(Incident).order_by(Incident.id.desc()).all()
    db.close()
    return [{
        "id": r.id,
        "service": r.service,
        "incident_type": r.incident_type,
        "severity": r.severity,
        "evidence": json.loads(r.evidence_json),
        "diagnosis": r.diagnosis,
        "status": r.status,
        "created_at": r.created_at,
        "closed_at": r.closed_at,
    } for r in rows]

def list_actions() -> List[Dict]:
    db = SessionLocal()
    rows = db.query(Action).order_by(Action.id.desc()).all()
    db.close()
    return [{
        "id": r.id,
        "incident_id": r.incident_id,
        "service": r.service,
        "action_type": r.action_type,
        "parameters": json.loads(r.parameters_json),
        "approval_required": r.approval_required,
        "approval_status": r.approval_status,
        "execution_status": r.execution_status,
        "created_at": r.created_at,
        "reviewed_by": r.reviewed_by,
        "reviewed_at": r.reviewed_at,
        "executed_by": r.executed_by,
        "executed_at": r.executed_at,
        "result": json.loads(r.result_json) if r.result_json else {},
    } for r in rows]

def update_action_approval(action_id: int, reviewer: str, decision: str, comment: str):
    db = SessionLocal()
    row = db.query(Action).filter(Action.id == action_id).first()
    if not row:
        db.close()
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Action not found")
    row.approval_status = decision
    row.execution_status = "ready" if decision == "approved" else "cancelled"
    row.reviewed_by = reviewer
    row.reviewed_at = now_iso()
    row.result_json = json.dumps({"review_comment": comment})
    db.commit()
    db.close()

def update_action_execution(action_id: int, executor: str, status: str, result: Dict):
    db = SessionLocal()
    row = db.query(Action).filter(Action.id == action_id).first()
    if not row:
        db.close()
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Action not found")
    row.execution_status = status
    row.executed_by = executor
    row.executed_at = now_iso()
    row.result_json = json.dumps(result)
    db.commit()
    db.close()

def close_incident(incident_id: int):
    db = SessionLocal()
    row = db.query(Incident).filter(Incident.id == incident_id).first()
    if row:
        row.status = "closed"
        row.closed_at = now_iso()
        db.commit()
    db.close()

def rolling_zscore(values: List[float]) -> float:
    arr = np.array(values, dtype=float)
    if len(arr) < 3:
        return 0.0
    baseline = arr[:-1]
    std = np.std(baseline)
    if std == 0:
        return 0.0
    return float((arr[-1] - np.mean(baseline)) / (std + 1e-9))

def classify_service(service_df: pd.DataFrame) -> List[Dict]:
    service = service_df["service"].iloc[0]
    incidents = []

    cpu_series = service_df[service_df["metric_name"] == "cpu_percent"]["metric_value"].tolist()
    err_series = service_df[service_df["metric_name"] == "error_rate"]["metric_value"].tolist()
    lat_series = service_df[service_df["metric_name"] == "latency_ms"]["metric_value"].tolist()

    cpu = cpu_series[-1] if cpu_series else None
    err = err_series[-1] if err_series else None
    lat = lat_series[-1] if lat_series else None

    cpu_z = rolling_zscore(cpu_series) if cpu_series else 0.0
    err_z = rolling_zscore(err_series) if err_series else 0.0
    lat_z = rolling_zscore(lat_series) if lat_series else 0.0

    if cpu is not None and (cpu >= CPU_THRESHOLD or cpu_z >= 2.5):
        incidents.append({
            "service": service,
            "incident_type": "cpu_saturation",
            "severity": "low" if cpu < 95 else "medium",
            "evidence": {"cpu_percent": cpu, "cpu_zscore": round(cpu_z, 2), "threshold": CPU_THRESHOLD},
            "diagnosis": f"CPU saturation detected for {service}",
        })

    if err is not None and (err >= ERROR_RATE_THRESHOLD or err_z >= 2.5):
        incidents.append({
            "service": service,
            "incident_type": "error_rate_spike",
            "severity": "medium" if err < 10 else "high",
            "evidence": {"error_rate": err, "error_zscore": round(err_z, 2), "threshold": ERROR_RATE_THRESHOLD},
            "diagnosis": f"Error-rate spike detected for {service}",
        })

    if lat is not None and (lat >= LATENCY_MS_THRESHOLD or lat_z >= 2.5):
        incidents.append({
            "service": service,
            "incident_type": "latency_degradation",
            "severity": "low" if lat < 2500 else "medium",
            "evidence": {"latency_ms": lat, "latency_zscore": round(lat_z, 2), "threshold": LATENCY_MS_THRESHOLD},
            "diagnosis": f"Latency degradation detected for {service}",
        })

    if err is not None and lat is not None and err >= ERROR_RATE_THRESHOLD and lat >= LATENCY_MS_THRESHOLD:
        incidents.append({
            "service": service,
            "incident_type": "release_regression_suspected",
            "severity": "high",
            "evidence": {"error_rate": err, "latency_ms": lat},
            "diagnosis": f"Combined error-rate and latency regression suspected for {service}",
        })

    return incidents

def plan_action(incident: Dict) -> Dict:
    incident_type = incident["incident_type"]
    service = incident["service"]
    severity = incident["severity"]

    approval_required = severity in ["medium", "high"]
    if severity == "low" and AUTO_APPROVE_LOW:
        approval_required = False

    if incident_type in ["cpu_saturation", "latency_degradation"]:
        return {
            "service": service,
            "action_type": "scale_up",
            "parameters": {"deployment": service, "replicas_delta": 1, "namespace": DEFAULT_NAMESPACE},
            "approval_required": approval_required,
        }

    if incident_type == "error_rate_spike":
        return {
            "service": service,
            "action_type": "restart_service",
            "parameters": {"deployment": service, "namespace": DEFAULT_NAMESPACE},
            "approval_required": approval_required,
        }

    if incident_type == "release_regression_suspected":
        return {
            "service": service,
            "action_type": "rollback_release",
            "parameters": {"deployment": service, "namespace": DEFAULT_NAMESPACE},
            "approval_required": True,
        }

    return {
        "service": service,
        "action_type": "observe_only",
        "parameters": {"deployment": service, "namespace": DEFAULT_NAMESPACE},
        "approval_required": True,
    }

def run_command(cmd: List[str]) -> Dict:
    try:
        proc = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, check=True)
        return {"success": True, "stdout": proc.stdout, "stderr": proc.stderr, "cmd": cmd}
    except subprocess.CalledProcessError as e:
        return {"success": False, "stdout": e.stdout, "stderr": e.stderr, "cmd": cmd}

def execute_remediation(action: Dict) -> Dict:
    action_type = action["action_type"]
    params = action["parameters"]
    deployment = params.get("deployment", "")
    namespace = params.get("namespace", DEFAULT_NAMESPACE)

    if not ENABLE_REAL_REMEDIATION:
        return {
            "mode": "dry_run",
            "success": True,
            "message": f"Simulated {action_type} for {deployment}",
            "parameters": params,
        }

    if action_type == "restart_service":
        return run_command(["kubectl", "rollout", "restart", f"deployment/{deployment}", "-n", namespace])

    if action_type == "rollback_release":
        return run_command(["kubectl", "rollout", "undo", f"deployment/{deployment}", "-n", namespace])

    if action_type == "scale_up":
        current = run_command(["kubectl", "get", f"deployment/{deployment}", "-n", namespace, "-o", "jsonpath={.spec.replicas}"])
        if not current["success"]:
            return current
        desired = int(current["stdout"] or "1") + int(params.get("replicas_delta", 1))
        return run_command(["kubectl", "scale", f"deployment/{deployment}", f"--replicas={desired}", "-n", namespace])

    return {"mode": "noop", "success": False, "message": f"Unsupported action {action_type}"}

def enqueue_action(action_id: int):
    if redis_client is None:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail="Redis not available")
    redis_client.rpush("autonomous:actions", json.dumps({"action_id": action_id}))

def process_one_queue_item(actor: str = "worker") -> Dict:
    if redis_client is None:
        return {"status": "redis_unavailable"}
    raw = redis_client.lpop("autonomous:actions")
    if not raw:
        return {"status": "empty"}
    payload = json.loads(raw)
    action_id = int(payload["action_id"])
    target = next((a for a in list_actions() if a["id"] == action_id), None)
    if not target:
        return {"status": "missing_action", "action_id": action_id}
    if target["approval_required"] and target["approval_status"] != "approved":
        return {"status": "not_approved", "action_id": action_id}
    result = execute_remediation(target)
    status = "executed" if result.get("success") else "failed"
    update_action_execution(action_id, actor, status, result)
    ACTIONS_EXECUTED.labels(result=status).inc()
    if status == "executed":
        close_incident(target["incident_id"])
    jlog("action_processed_from_queue", actor, {"action_id": action_id, "status": status})
    return {"status": status, "action_id": action_id, "result": result}

app = FastAPI(title=APP_TITLE, version=APP_VERSION)
app.add_middleware(
    CORSMiddleware,
    allow_origins=["*"],
    allow_credentials=True,
    allow_methods=["*"],
    allow_headers=["*"],
)

@app.middleware("http")
async def metrics_middleware(request, call_next):
    start = time.time()
    response = await call_next(request)
    duration = time.time() - start
    REQUEST_COUNT.labels(method=request.method, endpoint=request.url.path, status=response.status_code).inc()
    REQUEST_LATENCY.labels(endpoint=request.url.path).observe(duration)
    return response

    if not role:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="User not found")
    token = create_token(user, role)
    jlog("login", user, {"role": role})
    return {"token": token, "user": user, "role": role}

@app.get("/telemetry/recent")
def telemetry_recent(session: Dict = Depends(require_session)):

    return df.to_dict(orient="records")

@app.post("/autonomous/evaluate")
def autonomous_evaluate(session: Dict = Depends(require_session)):
    require_roles(session, ["admin", "operator", "compliance_reviewer"])
    AUTONOMOUS_EVALUATIONS.inc()
    df = fetch_recent_telemetry()
    if df.empty:
        return {"status": "no_data", "created_incidents": 0, "created_actions": 0}

    created_incidents = 0
    created_actions = 0

    for service, service_df in df.groupby("service"):
        incidents = classify_service(service_df)
        for incident in incidents:
            if unresolved_incident_exists(service, incident["incident_type"]):
                continue
            incident_id = create_incident(
                incident["service"],
                incident["incident_type"],
                incident["severity"],
                incident["evidence"],
                incident["diagnosis"],
            )
            created_incidents += 1

            planned = plan_action(incident)
            create_action(
                incident_id=incident_id,
                service=planned["service"],
                action_type=planned["action_type"],
                parameters=planned["parameters"],
                approval_required=planned["approval_required"],
            )
            created_actions += 1

            jlog("incident_created", session["sub"], {
                "incident_id": incident_id,
                "service": incident["service"],
                "incident_type": incident["incident_type"],
                "severity": incident["severity"],
            })

    return {"status": "evaluated", "created_incidents": created_incidents, "created_actions": created_actions}

@app.get("/incidents")
def incidents(session: Dict = Depends(require_session)):
    return list_incidents()

@app.get("/actions")
def actions(session: Dict = Depends(require_session)):
    return list_actions()

@app.post("/actions/{action_id}/approve")
def action_approve(action_id: int, payload: ApprovalIn, session: Dict = Depends(require_session)):
    require_roles(session, ["admin", "compliance_reviewer"])
    update_action_approval(action_id, session["sub"], payload.decision, payload.comment)
    jlog("action_reviewed", session["sub"], {"action_id": action_id, "decision": payload.decision, "comment": payload.comment})
    return {"status": payload.decision}

@app.post("/actions/{action_id}/execute")
def action_execute(action_id: int, payload: ExecuteIn, session: Dict = Depends(require_session)):
    require_roles(session, ["admin", "operator", "compliance_reviewer"])
    target = next((a for a in list_actions() if a["id"] == action_id), None)
    if not target:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Action not found")
    if target["approval_required"] and target["approval_status"] != "approved" and not payload.force:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Action requires approval")
    result = execute_remediation(target)
    status = "executed" if result.get("success") else "failed"
    update_action_execution(action_id, session["sub"], status, result)
    ACTIONS_EXECUTED.labels(result=status).inc()
    if status == "executed":
        close_incident(target["incident_id"])
    jlog("action_executed", session["sub"], {"action_id": action_id, "status": status, "result": result})
    return {"status": status, "result": result}

@app.post("/actions/{action_id}/enqueue")
def action_enqueue(action_id: int, session: Dict = Depends(require_session)):
    require_roles(session, ["admin", "operator", "compliance_reviewer"])
    enqueue_action(action_id)
    jlog("action_enqueued", session["sub"], {"action_id": action_id})
    return {"status": "queued", "action_id": action_id}

@app.post("/worker/process-once")
def worker_process_once(session: Dict = Depends(require_session)):
    require_roles(session, ["admin", "operator"])
    return process_one_queue_item(session["sub"])

@app.get("/dashboard/summary")
def dashboard_summary(session: Dict = Depends(require_session)):
    inc = list_incidents()
    act = list_actions()
    tele = fetch_recent_telemetry()
    return {
        "total_incidents": len(inc),
        "open_incidents": sum(1 for i in inc if i["status"] in ["open", "under_review"]),
        "high_incidents": sum(1 for i in inc if i["severity"] == "high"),
        "medium_incidents": sum(1 for i in inc if i["severity"] == "medium"),
        "low_incidents": sum(1 for i in inc if i["severity"] == "low"),
        "total_actions": len(act),
        "approved_actions": sum(1 for a in act if a["approval_status"] == "approved"),
        "pending_actions": sum(1 for a in act if a["approval_status"] == "pending"),
        "executed_actions": sum(1 for a in act if a["execution_status"] == "executed"),
        "failed_actions": sum(1 for a in act if a["execution_status"] == "failed"),
        "services_monitored": int(tele["service"].nunique()) if not tele.empty else 0,
    }

@app.get("/dashboard/charts")
def dashboard_charts(session: Dict = Depends(require_session)):
    df = fetch_recent_telemetry()
    if df.empty:
        return {"cpu_percent": [], "error_rate": [], "latency_ms": []}
    df["ts"] = pd.to_datetime(df["ts"])
    out = {}
    for metric in ["cpu_percent", "error_rate", "latency_ms"]:
        out[metric] = df[df["metric_name"] == metric][["ts", "service", "metric_value"]].to_dict(orient="records")
    return out

        jlog("argocd_sync", session["sub"], {"app": ARGOCD_APP, "stdout": proc.stdout})
        return {"status": "executed", "stdout": proc.stdout, "stderr": proc.stderr}
    except subprocess.CalledProcessError as e:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail={"stdout": e.stdout, "stderr": e.stderr})

import os
import pandas as pd
import requests
import streamlit as st

BACKEND_URL = os.getenv("BACKEND_URL", "http://backend:8000")

def api_get(path, token="", params=None, timeout=60):
    headers = {"X-Session-Token": token} if token else {}
    return requests.get(f"{BACKEND_URL}{path}", headers=headers, params=params or {}, timeout=timeout)

def api_post(path, token="", body=None, timeout=120):
    headers = {"X-Session-Token": token} if token else {}
    return requests.post(f"{BACKEND_URL}{path}", headers=headers, json=body or {}, timeout=timeout)

            st.session_state["user"] = data["user"]
            st.session_state["role"] = data["role"]
        else:
            st.error(r.text)

    if st.session_state["token"]:
        st.success(f"{st.session_state['user']} ({st.session_state['role']})")

token = st.session_state["token"]
if not token:
    st.stop()

tabs = st.tabs(["Overview", "Telemetry", "Incidents & Actions", "Boardroom", "GitOps"])

with tabs[0]:
    health = api_get("/health", token=token)
    if health.status_code == 200:
        st.json(health.json())

    summary = api_get("/dashboard/summary", token=token)
    if summary.status_code == 200:
        s = summary.json()
        c1, c2, c3, c4 = st.columns(4)
        c1.metric("Open Incidents", s["open_incidents"])
        c2.metric("High Incidents", s["high_incidents"])
        c3.metric("Pending Actions", s["pending_actions"])
        c4.metric("Executed Actions", s["executed_actions"])

    a1, a2 = st.columns(2)
    with a1:
        if st.button("Generate Sample Telemetry", use_container_width=True):
            st.write(api_post("/telemetry/sample", token=token).json())
    with a2:
        if st.button("Run Autonomous Evaluation", use_container_width=True):
            st.write(api_post("/autonomous/evaluate", token=token).json())

with tabsst.subheader("Manual Telemetry")
    c1, c2, c3 = st.columns(3)
    with c1:
        service = st.text_input("Service", value="orders-api")
    with c2:
        metric_name = st.selectbox("Metric", ["cpu_percent", "error_rate", "latency_ms"])
    with c3:
        metric_value = st.number_input("Metric value", value=90.0)

    if st.button("Send Telemetry", use_container_width=True):
        st.write(api_post("/telemetry", token=token, body={
            "service": service,
            "metric_name": metric_name,
            "metric_value": metric_value
        }).json())

    st.markdown("---")
    recent = api_get("/telemetry/recent", token=token)
    if recent.status_code == 200:
        df = pd.DataFrame(recent.json())
        st.dataframe(df, use_container_width=True, hide_index=True)

with tabs[2]:
    inc = api_get("/incidents", token=token)
    act = api_get("/actions", token=token)

    if inc.status_code == 200:
        st.subheader("Incidents")
        inc_df = pd.DataFrame(inc.json())
        st.dataframe(inc_df, use_container_width=True, hide_index=True)

    if act.status_code == 200:
        st.subheader("Actions")
        act_df = pd.DataFrame(act.json())
        st.dataframe(act_df, use_container_width=True, hide_index=True)
        if not act_df.empty:
            action_id = st.selectbox("Select action ID", act_df["id"].tolist())
            r1, r2, r3, r4 = st.columns(4)
            with r1:
                if st.button("Approve", use_container_width=True):
                    st.write(api_post(f"/actions/{action_id}/approve", token=token, body={"decision": "approved", "comment": "Approved from UI"}).json())
            with r2:
                if st.button("Reject", use_container_width=True):
                    st.write(api_post(f"/actions/{action_id}/approve", token=token, body={"decision": "rejected", "comment": "Rejected from UI"}).json())
            with r3:
                if st.button("Execute", use_container_width=True):
                    st.write(api_post(f"/actions/{action_id}/execute", token=token, body={"force": False}).json())
            with r4:
                if st.button("Queue", use_container_width=True):
                    st.write(api_post(f"/actions/{action_id}/enqueue", token=token).json())

with tabs[3]:
    summary = api_get("/dashboard/summary", token=token)
    charts = api_get("/dashboard/charts", token=token)
    if summary.status_code == 200:
        s = summary.json()
        m1, m2, m3, m4 = st.columns(4)
        m1.metric("Services", s["services_monitored"])
        m2.metric("Incidents", s["total_incidents"])
        m3.metric("Actions", s["total_actions"])
        m4.metric("Approved", s["approved_actions"])

    if charts.status_code == 200:
        payload = charts.json()
        for metric in ["cpu_percent", "error_rate", "latency_ms"]:
            df = pd.DataFrame(payload.get(metric, []))
            if not df.empty:
                df["ts"] = pd.to_datetime(df["ts"])
                st.markdown(f"**{metric}**")
                st.line_chart(df.pivot(index="ts", columns="service", values="metric_value"))

with tabs[4]:
    if st.button("Sync ArgoCD App", use_container_width=True):
        st.write(api_post("/gitops/argocd/sync", token=token).json())

    if st.button("Process One Queue Item", use_container_width=True):
        st.write(api_post("/worker/process-once", token=token).json())
EOF

cat > scripts/worker_loop.sh <<'EOF'
#!/usr/bin/env sh
set -eu

TOKEN=$(python - <<'PY'
import requests
r = requests.get("http://backend:8000/auth/login/operator", timeout=30)
print(r.json()["token"])
PY
)

while true; do
  wget -qO- --post-data='{}' --header="Content-Type: application/json" --header="X-Session-Token: $TOKEN" http://backend:8000/worker/process-once || true
  sleep 5
done
EOF

chmod +x scripts/worker_loop.sh

cat > Dockerfile <<'EOF'
FROM python:3.11-slim

WORKDIR /app
COPY requirements.txt /app/requirements.txt
RUN pip install --no-cache-dir --upgrade pip && pip install --no-cache-dir -r /app/requirements.txt

COPY backend /app/backend
COPY frontend /app/frontend
COPY scripts /app/scripts
COPY .env /app/.env
RUN mkdir -p /app/data /app/logs && chmod +x /app/scripts/worker_loop.sh

EXPOSE 8000 8501
EOF

cat > prometheus.yml <<'EOF'
global:
  scrape_interval: 15s

scrape_configs:
  - job_name: 'autonomous-backend'
    metrics_path: /metrics
    static_configs:
      - targets: ['backend:8000']
EOF

cat > docker-compose.yml <<'EOF'
services:
  postgres:
    image: postgres:16
    container_name: autonomous-postgres
    environment:
      POSTGRES_USER: autonomous
      POSTGRES_PASSWORD: autonomous
      POSTGRES_DB: autonomous
    ports:
      - "5432:5432"
    volumes:
      - postgres_data:/var/lib/postgresql/data

  redis:
    image: redis:7
    container_name: autonomous-redis
    ports:
      - "6379:6379"

  zookeeper:
    image: confluentinc/cp-zookeeper:7.6.1
    container_name: autonomous-zookeeper
    environment:
      ZOOKEEPER_CLIENT_PORT: 2181
      ZOOKEEPER_TICK_TIME: 2000

  kafka:
    image: confluentinc/cp-kafka:7.6.1
    container_name: autonomous-kafka
    depends_on:
      - zookeeper
    ports:
      - "9092:9092"
    environment:
      KAFKA_BROKER_ID: 1
      KAFKA_ZOOKEEPER_CONNECT: zookeeper:2181
      KAFKA_LISTENERS: PLAINTEXT://0.0.0.0:9092
      KAFKA_ADVERTISED_LISTENERS: PLAINTEXT://kafka:9092
      KAFKA_OFFSETS_TOPIC_REPLICATION_FACTOR: 1

  backend:
    build: .
    container_name: autonomous-backend
    env_file:
      - .env
    command: uvicorn backend.main:app --host 0.0.0.0 --port 8000
    depends_on:
      - postgres
      - redis
      - kafka
    ports:
      - "8000:8000"
    volumes:
      - ./logs:/app/logs
      - ./data:/app/data

  frontend:
    build: .
    container_name: autonomous-frontend
    env_file:
      - .env
    command: streamlit run frontend/Home.py --server.port 8501 --server.address 0.0.0.0
    depends_on:
      - backend
    ports:
      - "8501:8501"

  worker:
    build: .
    container_name: autonomous-worker
    env_file:
      - .env
    command: /app/scripts/worker_loop.sh
    depends_on:
      - backend
      - redis

  prometheus:
    image: prom/prometheus:v2.54.1
    container_name: autonomous-prometheus
    command:
      - "--config.file=/etc/prometheus/prometheus.yml"
    volumes:
      - ./prometheus.yml:/etc/prometheus/prometheus.yml:ro
    depends_on:
      - backend
    ports:
      - "9090:9090"

  grafana:
    image: grafana/grafana:11.1.4
    container_name: autonomous-grafana
    depends_on:
      - prometheus
    ports:
      - "3000:3000"

volumes:
  postgres_data:
EOF

echo
echo "READY: $ROOT"
echo "start: docker compose up --build"
echo "frontend: http://127.0.0.1:8501"
echo "backend:  http://127.0.0.1:8000"
echo "prometheus: http://127.0.0.1:9090"
echo "grafana: http://127.0.0.1:3000"

Start
cd "$HOME/autonomous-platform-docker"
docker compose up --build
====
